Hardware evaluations span six distinct outdoor environments, with two representative executions presented in the paper and four additional executions included in the supplementary video. The evaluation also reports the accuracy--latency trade-off for the Flow Matching warm start.

</details>


### [160] [LBDU-VIO: Learned Bias Dynamics and Uncertainty for Visual-Inertial Odometry with Unreliable Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39125)
*Qizhi Guo,Junning Lyu,Defu Lin,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LBDU-VIO，一种学习增强的MSCKF：用神经常微分方程建模连续时间零偏动态以替代随机游走模型，并用IMU不确定性模型预测运动自适应噪声协方差；两者仅用位姿监督训练，在EuRoC与TUM-VI上误差低于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人VIO依赖视觉更新间的高频IMU递推，但传统多状态约束卡尔曼滤波（MSCKF）采用随机游走零偏假设与固定噪声参数，当视觉信息不可靠时鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出LBDU-VIO，学习增强的MSCKF。其一，用神经常微分方程（ODE）建模连续时间零偏动态，用于滤波器零偏状态递推，取代随机游走模型；其二，构建IMU不确定性模型，预测运动自适应的量测噪声协方差以用于协方差递推。两个模型均在无直接标签条件下以位姿监督训练。

Result: 在真实世界EuRoC与TUM-VI基准上误差低于代表性视觉惯性基线；在存在10秒视觉中断的EuRoC序列上，平均相对位置误差较S-MSCKF降低25.1%。

Conclusion: 以学习方式替代传统零偏随机游走与固定噪声假设，可有效提升VIO在视觉不可靠场景下的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) for aerial robots relies on high rate inertial measurement unit (IMU) propagation between visual updates. However, conventional multi state constraint Kalman filters (MSCKFs) use random walk bias assumptions and fixed noise parameters, which can limit robustness when visual information is unreliable. To address this problem, we propose LBDU-VIO, a learning-augmented MSCKF with learned continuous time bias dynamics and an IMU uncertainty model. A neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) models continuous time bias dynamics to propagate the filter's bias states, replacing their random walk model. The IMU uncertainty model predicts motion adaptive measurement noise covariances for covariance propagation. Both models are trained with pose supervision without direct labels. Experiments on real world EuRoC and TUM-VI benchmarks show lower errors than representative visual-inertial baselines, including a 25.1% reduction in mean relative position error compared with S-MSCKF on EuRoC sequences with 10s visual outage.

</details>


### [161] [Drape-Compatible Tool-Tip Localization for Hand-Held Laparoscopic Instruments via UWB Carrier-Phase Ranging and Trocar-Constrained Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39129)
*Jinseok Lee,Dongho Yee,Minsung Kim,Younghoon Noh,Seonho Shim,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jiyul Lee,Seong Jeong,Hyuk Choi,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仅用超宽带载波相位测距与IMU估计腹腔镜器械工具尖端位置的方法，借助套管针约束解决坐标不足与整周模糊，在体模与活体实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人策略需知器械工作端相对摄像头及彼此的位姿，但腹腔镜手术仅有内窥镜视频，且铺巾遮挡器械自身所有光学路径，视觉与光学追踪不可用。

Method: 在无菌屏障外的手柄侧布置超宽带天线节点（每把持械一件、内窥镜一件），以载波相位测距，内窥镜天线为参考。三对天线仅给三个距离而器械天线有六个坐标，缺三个自由度；载波相位每对另引入一个整周未知数，距离仅以λ/2=23.1 mm为模确定。利用套管针约束：每根器械杆穿过位置已知的孔，方向由机上IMU测得，使天线只剩插入深度一个自由度；两个未知数对应三个距离，两个定深度、第三个作为冗余方程暴露错误整周或安装偏移。同一求解同时给出内窥镜坐标系下两枚工具尖端，无需第二载波频率解整周。

Result: 11孔体模含金属器械下三路距离σ=0.10–0.33 mm，更新率35–46 Hz；无菌铺巾压在天线上链路不受影响（光学路径则会被阻断）；记录仪被带入活体兔阑尾切除术。

Conclusion: 套管针几何约束将欠定问题转为可解并自带一致性校验，无需双频即可在单PLL锁定下完成整周判定，实现遮挡鲁棒的器械尖端定位。

Abstract: A surgical robot policy needs to know where each instrument's working end sits relative to the camera and to the other instrument, yet a laparoscopic operation leaves only the endoscope video, and a draped instrument hides every optical path on itself. We estimate the tool tips from distances and an IMU alone. The distances are measured by ultra-wideband carrier phase between antenna nodes on the handle side of the instruments: one per hand-held instrument, one on the endoscope, all outside the sterile barrier. Take the endoscope antenna as the reference. The two instrument antennas carry six coordinates while the three pairs give three distances, so the problem is short by three, and averaging cannot close that gap because what is missing is information, not precision. Carrier phase adds one more unknown per pair, an integer that leaves distance fixed only modulo lambda/2 = 23.1 mm. The trocar settles both difficulties: each shaft passes through a port whose position is known and whose direction the on-board IMU measures, so its antenna keeps a single degree of freedom, the insertion depth. Two unknowns now face three distances -- two fix the depths and the third is left over, and that spare equation exposes a wrong integer or a wrong mounting offset instead of absorbing it. The same solve returns both tool tips in the endoscope frame, without the second carrier frequency that integer resolution usually needs, so the link never leaves a single PLL lock. In an 11-hole phantom among metal instruments the three distances hold sigma = 0.10-0.33 mm at 35-46 Hz, a sterile drape pressed onto the antennas leaves the link unchanged where an optical path would be blocked, and the logger was carried into a live rabbit appendectomy.

</details>


### [162] [Blackout vs. Freeze: Analyzing Physical Failure Modes of VLAs under Camera Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39145)
*Heejae Suh,Jongwook Han,Zahra Gholami,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文分析VLA模型（π0.5与GR00T）在视觉输入故障下的物理失效模式，发现黑屏与冻结虽任务成功率相近但失效模式不同，并评估两种缓解方法及其副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉输入不可靠会损害视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的任务表现，并带来潜在物理安全风险；需理解不同视觉故障如何导致具体物理失效，以便设计更安全的策略。

Method: 分析π0.5与GR00T在图像黑屏、冻结等输入故障下的行为；比较不同故障的物理失效模式；开展选择性干预实验，考察本体感觉（当前机器人状态）与腕部/场景视图信息的作用；评估相机黑屏训练与免训练替换故障视觉嵌入两种缓解方法；并进行真实机器人试验。

Result: 黑屏与冻结在任务成功率同样低的情况下产生不同的物理失效模式：冻结导致更极端的关节行为，夹爪闭合后黑屏更易导致物体掉落，在无本体感觉时尤为明显。本体感觉可部分补偿缺失的机器人图像信息并减少非目标接触，但在腕部视图物体信息被移除时，即使有剩余场景视图也难以充分恢复任务成功。两种缓解方法在特定条件下提升成功率，但可能增加对周围物体的非预期接触或扰动；真实机器人试验显示相机故障下成功执行仍可能伴随非预期物理交互。

Conclusion: 应设计在相机故障下仍能利用可用机器人与物体信息的VLA策略，以限制危险运动。

Abstract: Unreliable visual inputs can harm task performance and cause potential physical safety risks for vision-language-action (VLA) models. We analyze how $π0.5$ and GR00T models act under input faults such as image blackouts and freezing. We find that blackout and freezing produce distinct physical failure modes even when task-success rates are similarly low: freezing causes more extreme joint behavior, whereas blackout after gripper closure can cause more object drops, most markedly without proprioception. Selective intervention studies reveal that proprioception (current robot state) partly compensates for the removed robot depictions and reduces non-target contact. However, it cannot sufficiently restore task success when wrist-view object information is removed, even when aided by the remaining scene view. We then evaluate two mitigation approaches: camera-blackout training and training-free replacement of faulty visual embeddings. Both improve task success in selected conditions, but can increase unintended contact or disturbance to surrounding objects. Real-robot trials further show that successful execution under camera faults can still involve unintended physical interactions. These findings motivate designing VLA policies that use the robot and object information still available under camera faults to limit hazardous motion.

</details>


### [163] [Linear Recurrent Memory Suffices to Distil a World-Model Policy for Robot Air Hockey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39151)
*F. Olivia Fan,Oliver Obst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在模拟空气曲棍球追踪丢失任务中，线性递归记忆（k=0）即可匹配GRU与教师策略，非线性递归动态并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究记忆依赖型控制任务是否必须依赖非线性递归动态：现有递归策略（如GRU）默认使用非线性状态更新，但尚不清楚非线性递归动态本身是否为记忆能力的必要条件。

Method: 以DreamerV3为教师，在追踪丢失条件下训练；通过重置教师递归状态验证任务的记忆依赖性；将教师蒸馏为64维状态的紧凑递归策略，采用对角线性递归加可选秩-k非线性创新项，同时保留非线性观测编码器与动作头；在五个配对随机种子下比较纯线性递归（k=0）、GRU基线与教师，并在追踪丢失范围内测试；最终评估在冻结模型与分析后才开启的全新测试划分上进行。

Result: DreamerV3教师优于无记忆策略，重置其递归状态后性能显著下降，证明任务需要记忆；纯线性递归模型（k=0）在测试的追踪丢失范围内匹配GRU基线与教师；提高非线性创新秩未带来可测量的收益；线性模型所需递归参数更少、计算量更低，但性能相当。

Conclusion: 在该记忆依赖型控制任务中，围绕简单线性记忆机制进行非线性表征学习已足够，非线性递归动态并非必需。结论受限于所考虑的模拟任务、教师、状态维度、黑屏时长，以及观测编码器与动作头仍保持非线性的策略设定。

Abstract: Does memory-dependent control need nonlinear recurrent dynamics? We study simulated air-hockey defence under temporary loss of puck tracking. A DreamerV3 teacher outperforms a memoryless policy under tracking loss, while resetting the teacher's recurrent state sharply reduces performance, which demonstrates that the task requires memory. We distil this teacher into compact recurrent policies with a 64 dimensional state, with a combination of a diagonal linear recurrence and an optional rank-$k$ nonlinear innovation while retaining nonlinear observation encoders and action heads. Across five matched seeds, the purely linear recurrent model ($k=0$) matches both the GRU baseline and the teacher throughout the tested range of tracking loss. Increasing nonlinear innovation rank providing no measured benefits. This result is obtained on a fresh test split, which will be only opened after all models and analyses are frozen. The linear model requires fewer recurrent parameters and less computation than GRU, but performs comparably. These results suggest that, for this memory dependent control task, nonlinear representation learning around a simple linear memory mechanism can be sufficient, and that nonlinear recurrent dynamics are not necessarily required. These conclusions are limited to the simulated task, teacher, state dimension, and blackout horizon considered here, and to policies whose observation encoder and action head remain nonlinear.

</details>


### [164] [Concurrent Semantic Search and Mission Execution for LTL Missions in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39153)
*Fernando Salanova Gaspar,David Morilla Cabello,Cristian Mahulea Poleuca,Eduardo Montijano Muñoz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应高层规划方法，将任务驱动的语义搜索与LTLf任务执行交织进行，以实现非短视的探索与任务推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LTLf任务求解方法通常假设环境已知，或把环境探索与任务执行分离；语义探索方法则一次只寻找一个目标且忽略正在执行的任务。在未知环境中执行复杂任务时，机器人需要同时推理"该做什么"和"还需发现什么"，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 方法在线构建两种表示：(1) 基于视觉语言模型(VLM)构建的度量-语义场景图，用于定位任务所指对象的证据；(2) 编码任务的确定性有限自动机(DFA)，用于指示每个任务状态下哪些对象重要。在每个规划阶段，规划器选择对语义搜索和任务推进都最有价值的航点，并针对剩余任务阶段进行估值以避免阻塞状态；所选航点被排序为单一高层规划，并随新信息到达而重新计算。

Result: 在逼真室内环境的五类任务上，该方法比对比方法完成更多任务，同时需要覆盖的环境更少，路径更短，并遵守任务施加的约束。

Conclusion: 通过将任务驱动的语义搜索与任务执行以非短视方式交织，并在线更新场景图与DFA表示，本方法在未知环境中实现了更高效、更合规的复杂任务规划。

Abstract: Planning complex missions in unknown environments requires robots to reason simultaneously about what they should do and what they still need to discover. Existing approaches for solving LTLf missions typically assume a known environment, or separate the exploration of the environment from the execution of the mission, while semantic exploration methods look for one target at a time and ignore the mission being executed.
  To fill this gap, our main contribution is an adaptive high-level planning method that interleaves a task-driven semantic search with the execution of the mission, advancing both in a non-myopic manner.
  Our method leverages two representations built online, a metric-semantic scene graph, built with a Vision Language Model (VLM), that provides the evidence needed to locate the objects the mission refers to, and the deterministic finite automaton (DFA) encoding the mission, that indicates which of them matter at each mission state. At every planning stage, our planner selects the waypoints that are most valuable for both the semantic search and the advancement of the mission, valuing them over the remaining mission stages in order to avoid blocking states. The selected waypoints are then ordered in a single high-level plan, which is recomputed as new information arrives.
  In photorealistic indoor environments over five mission types, our method completes more missions than the compared approaches while having to cover less of the environment, and it does so with shorter paths and complying with the restrictions imposed by the mission.

</details>


### [165] [Exploiting Vulnerabilities: Universal Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39178)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuanwei Liu,Xuetao Li,Xuanye Fei,He Huang,Zheng Wang,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"通用对抗物体"——一种表面纹理经过优化的球体，放入机器人视野即可显著降低VLA模型的任务成功率；该多层级攻击同时干扰轨迹规划、任务执行与动作控制，在仿真与真实机器人上验证，使Pi0和RDT成功率下降31.2%–39.9%，复杂场景下接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉感知、语言理解与动作生成整合进端到端框架，直接与物理世界和人类交互，其安全性至关重要；即使微小漏洞也可能导致灾难性失败，因此需要研究针对VLA的对抗攻击以揭示其脆弱性。

Method: 提出"通用对抗物体"：一个表面纹理经优化的球体，置于机器人视野内即可生效。方法上构建多层级攻击框架，联合破坏轨迹规划、任务执行与动作控制三个环节，并在仿真环境和真实机器人平台上进行验证。

Result: 在两个代表性VLA模型（Pi0与RDT）上，该对抗物体使平均任务成功率下降31.2%–39.9%；在复杂场景中，成功率降至接近零。

Conclusion: 研究表明VLA模型存在严重的对抗脆弱性，一个纹理优化的球体即可造成跨模型的通用攻击效果，凸显了机器人安全防护的迫切性，并为后续鲁棒性研究提供了攻击基准。

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have revolutionized robotic manipulation by seamlessly integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation in an end-to-end learning framework. However, since these models are designed to interact directly with the physical world and humans, their security is critical, and even small vulnerabilities can lead to catastrophic failures. In this work, we propose the Universal Adversarial Object, a sphere with optimized surface texture that significantly degrades task success rates when placed within the robot's field of view. Specifically, our approach introduces a multi-level attack framework that jointly disrupts trajectory planning, task execution, and action control. We validate our method in both simulated and real-world robotic settings. Experimental results demonstrate that the adversarial object reduces the average task success rates by 31.2%-39.9% for two representative VLA models (Pi0 and RDT), with success rates dropping to near zero in complex scenarios. Index Terms--Vision-Language-Action models, adversarial attack, robotic security, universal adversarial object

</details>


### [166] [LocoWM: High-Precision Locomotion through World-Model-Guided Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39179)
*Zijie Zhao,Shengqian Chen,Xiaoxu Wang,Han Jiang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LocoWM，一种基于世界模型的预主动残差自适应框架，用于高精度运动控制，通过预测未来物理状态生成动作修正，在多个任务上优于端到端与反应式残差基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度运动需要将运动指令跟踪与任务相关物理状态的精确调节相结合。端到端联合优化会导致精度目标优化不足，而反应式残差控制仅在偏差可观测后才调整动作，存在滞后。因此需要一种能预先补偿预期偏差的方法。

Method: LocoWM框架包含三部分：(1) 基础策略（base policy）实现指令跟随运动；(2) 动作条件世界模型，基于本体感知历史与拟议基础动作预测未来物理状态序列；(3) 残差适配器，以预测状态序列为条件生成加性动作修正，补偿预期偏差。采用两阶段训练：先学习运动与动作条件动力学，再冻结这两个模块训练适配器，实现运动习得与精度自适应的解耦。

Result: 在terrain leveling（地形平整）、acceleration compensation（加速度补偿）与push recovery（推力恢复）实验中，相比端到端和反应式残差基线，控制精度与扰动鲁棒性均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过世界模型预测未来状态驱动的预主动残差自适应，可有效提升高精度运动的控制精度与抗扰动能力，两阶段训练策略成功分离了运动习得与精度自适应。

Abstract: High-precision locomotion combines motion-command tracking with precise regulation of task-relevant physical states, enabling robots to interact reliably with their surroundings during motion. Joint end-to-end optimization can leave precision objectives insufficiently optimized, while reactive residual control adjusts actions only after deviations become observable. We present \textbf{LocoWM}, a world-model-guided preactive residual adaptation framework for high-precision locomotion. A base policy provides command-following locomotion, while an action-conditioned world model predicts a sequence of future physical states from proprioceptive history and the proposed base action. A residual adapter conditions on this predicted sequence to generate additive action corrections that compensate for anticipated deviations. Two-stage training first learns locomotion and action-conditioned dynamics, then freezes both modules while training the adapter, separating locomotion acquisition from precision adaptation. Experiments spanning terrain leveling, acceleration compensation, and push recovery demonstrate improved control precision and disturbance robustness over end-to-end and reactive residual baselines. Demos and code are available at: https://zhaozijie2022.github.io/LocoWM

</details>


### [167] [DSDyn-VLA: A Dual-Stream Dynamic Manipulation Framework with Motion Perception, Future Awareness, and Realtime Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39198)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yu Han,Yichao Cao,Shan You,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DSDyn-VLA慢快双流框架，结合运动感知预规划与实时残差修正，解决动态操作中VLA的感知、延迟与控制三大缺陷，并发布DynBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在静态任务表现优异，但在物体运动的动态环境（如传送带操作）中失效。作者识别出三大根本缺陷：感知差距（静态视觉缺时间运动线索）、延迟差距（推理延迟使动作过时）、控制差距（开环动作块执行缺实时调整）。

Method: 提出DSDyn-VLA慢快双流动态操作框架。慢流Flow-Planner作为宏观规划器，通过光流增强时序感知、利用未来状态感知机制预判并抵消推理延迟，生成全局一致、运动感知的动作块；快流Res-Refiner使用轻量级RL策略，基于实时观测向规划动作块注入高频闭环修正。同时构建基于MuJoCo的DynBench基准，含九个动态操作任务。

Result: 在Kinetix动态基准高延迟设定下，失败率较当前SOTA降低超76%；真实动态场景成功率约为PI0.5的6倍，DynBench上约为5倍。

Conclusion: DSDyn-VLA通过慢快双流架构有效弥补感知、延迟与控制三大差距，在动态操作任务中显著优于现有方法，并将开源全部代码与权重。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in static tasks, they struggle in dynamic environments where objects are in motion (e.g., conveyor belt manipulation). We identify three fundamental limitations hindering current VLAs in these scenarios: the \textbf{perception gap}, where static visual inputs lack temporal motion cues; the \textbf{latency gap}, where inference delays render actions obsolete; and the \textbf{control gap}, caused by the open-loop action chunk execution without real-time adjustment. In this work, we propose \textbf{DSDyn-VLA}, a Slow-Fast \textbf{D}ual-\textbf{S}tream \textbf{Dyn}amic manipulation framework that integrates motion-aware foresighted planning with real-time residual correction. The slow \textbf{Flow-Planner} serves as a macro-planner. By enhancing the VLA with optical flow for temporal perception and a future state awareness mechanism to preemptively offset inference latency, it produces globally consistent, motion-aware action chunks. Complementing this, the fast \textbf{Res-Refiner} employs a lightweight RL policy to inject high-frequency, closed-loop corrections into the planned action chunks based on real-time observations. In addition, we introduce \textbf{DynBench}, a MuJoCo-based benchmark for dynamic object manipulation that comprises nine tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSDyn-VLA reduces the failure rate by over 76\% compared to current SOTA method in high-latency setting on the Kinetix dynamic benchmark, while achieving about 6$\times$ the success rate of PI0.5 in real-world dynamic settings and about 5$\times$ on DynBench. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [168] [Benchmarking EMlog Calibration for Autonomous Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39203)
*Samuel Cohen-Salmon,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对电磁计程仪(EMLog)原始测量存在系统误差、缺乏动态海况下校准模型对比评估的问题，提出模型化校准方法，用两种估计流程对比四种校准模型，并在221分钟实船数据上验证。结果表明，采用卡尔曼滤波的偏置-尺度误差模型将速度估计精度提升71%，且动态机动优于直线路径，实现了面向自主水面车辆的实时在线EMLog校准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面与水下航行器需精确测速，通常依赖DVL，但受作业高度限制DVL会失效，此时EMLog是连续测速的关键替代方案。然而EMLog原始测量含系统误差，须在任务前校准；现有文献缺乏对动态海况下不同校准模型性能的基准对比评估。

Method: 提出基于模型的对比校准方法，构建两个不同的估计流程，对四种校准模型进行统一评估。使用MARVEL水面车辆在动态海试中采集的221分钟连续真实遥测数据进行严格验证，数据包含两套EMLog与DVL记录。

Result: 在偏置与尺度误差模型上结合卡尔曼滤波，速度估计精度提升71%；相比标准直线路径，动态机动能进一步提升精度，验证了面向自主水面车辆的实时在线EMLog校准方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了动态海况下EMLog校准模型缺乏基准对比的空白，证明了基于卡尔曼滤波的偏置-尺度误差模型在真实海试中的显著优越性，并提供了一套经验证、可实时在线的EMLog校准方案，可用于自主水面车辆。

Abstract: Accurate velocity measurement is a fundamental requirement for autonomous surface and underwater vehicles. Commonly, velocity is provided by a Doppler velocity log (DVL) sensor, yet it becomes unavailable due to operational altitude constraints. In such situations, electromagnetic logs (EMLogs) provide a critically robust alternative for continuous velocity estimation. However, raw EMLog measurements are inherently corrupted by systematic errors, which need to be calibrated prior mission begins. Currently, a benchmarking comparative evaluation of how different calibration models perform under rapidly changing dynamic sea conditions is missing in the literature. To bridge this gap, this paper presents a comparative model-based calibration methodology that evaluates four distinct calibration models using two different estimation pipelines. The proposed framework is rigorously validated on a unique 221 minutes of continuous real-world telemetry collected from the MARVEL surface vehicle during dynamic sea trials. The dataset contains two different EMLogs and DVL recordings. Experimental results demonstrate that the bias and scale error model implemented with the Kalman filter improves the speed estimation by 71%. We also demonstrate that dynamical manoeuvres further improve the accuracy compared to standard straight-line paths, ultimately delivering a validated, real-time online calibration EMLog approach for autonomous surface vehicles.

</details>


### [169] [ASENA: Self-evolving Agents for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39207)
*An-Chieh Cheng,Isabella Liu,Edmund Bu,Johan Bjorck,Hongxu Yin,Zhengyi Luo,Jan Kautz,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASENA是一个将通用编码智能体与机器人感知、计算、监督执行及持久经验相连的具身智能体系统；其内置的4B单目导航策略ASENA-VLN在R2R/RxR上达到68.7%/70.2%的SOTA，与编码智能体集成后成功率提升11个百分点，并经多轮工作区演化从72%/65%提升至98%/89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统多依赖预定义技能或固定策略，难以在线编写程序、诊断失败并积累可复用经验；本文希望让通用编码智能体直接驱动机器人，并将学习到的导航能力作为可选工具嵌入可编程框架，以突破单纯路线跟随与预设技能的限制。

Method: 提出ASENA具身智能体系统，让编码智能体编写并执行程序、检查记录结果、修复失败，并在模型权重固定的前提下复用笔记与可执行技能；同时提出ASENA-VLN，一个4B单目导航策略，使用共享视觉-语言解码器在路线指令、视觉问答及新构建的几何衍生原子导航任务数据集上训练，预测体坐标系轨迹（长路线与短时行为）。

Result: ASENA-VLN单独作为策略在R2R达68.7%成功率、RxR达70.2%；与编码智能体集成后，在两个agentic基准上成功率均提升11个百分点并缩短执行时间；通过持久工作区演化与模拟器反馈，在重复的100任务子集上迭代十轮，R2R从72%提升至98%，RxR从65%提升至89%；在具身问答上以更少交互步数取得SOTA准确率；Unitree G1真机演示无预建地图完成搜索、视觉检查、空间推理与手势合成。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在线编程与持久经验积累能有效扩展机器人行为边界，使系统超越路线跟随和预定义技能；学习到的导航策略作为可选工具可显著提升可编程智能体的成功率与效率，并在仿真与真实平台均具可行性。

Abstract: We present ASENA, an embodied agent system that connects general-purpose coding agents to robot sensing, computation, supervised execution, and persistent experience. Agents can write and execute programs, inspect recorded outcomes, repair failures, and reuse notes and executable skills while keeping their model weights fixed. We further introduce ASENA-VLN, a 4B monocular navigation policy that serves as an optional tool within this programmable system. ASENA-VLN predicts body-frame trajectories for both extended routes and short-horizon behaviors using a shared vision-language decoder trained on route instructions, visual question answering, and a newly curated dataset of geometry-derived atomic navigation tasks. As a standalone policy, ASENA-VLN achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 68.7% on R2R and 70.2% on RxR. When integrated with a coding agent, learned navigation improves ASENA's success rate by 11 percentage points on both agentic benchmarks while reducing execution time. Through persistent workspace evolution and simulator feedback, ten passes over recurring 100-task subsets further improve success from 72% to 98% on R2R and from 65% to 89% on RxR. On embodied question answering, ASENA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with fewer interaction steps. Finally, real-world demonstrations on a Unitree G1 combine search, visual inspection, spatial reasoning, and synthesized gestures without a pre-built map, illustrating how online programming extends robot behavior beyond route following and predefined skills.

</details>


### [170] [The Planning Limits of Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39235)
*Ali Alrasheed,Basim Azam,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了动作条件世界模型在机器人规划中的可用范围与失效边界：预测器仅在目标落在其想象轨迹内或略微超出时可靠，五步 rollout 只能可靠排序未来 5–10 控制步的动作，而任务目标通常在 16–53 步外；该限制在完美预测下依然存在，需更长想象轨迹或更近子目标，并可用于提升 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型研究多展示"能做什么"，而缺乏对其预测何时对规划有用、何时失效的边界刻画。作者希望明确世界模型的可规划范围，以指导其在机器人想象式规划中的合理使用。

Method: 在五个冻结自监督视觉骨干（V-JEPA 2、V-JEPA 2.1、VideoMAEv2、VideoPrism、DINOv2）上构建动作条件预测器，以测试可跨环境迁移的表征；在 Meta-World 多样化操作任务与 BridgeData V2 的真实机器人交互上评估，并对照真实模拟器进行完美预测实验，比较纯想象、MPC 反馈规划、想象至目标、专家子目标等策略，以及用世界模型筛选 VLA 候选动作。

Result: 五步 rollout（训练长度）下世界模型仅对前方 5–10 控制步的目标可靠排序，而任务目标在 16–53 步外；81 倍更大的预测器与更长 rollout 训练均未扩展该范围；编码器同时影响范围与闭环成功率，V-JEPA 2.1 表现最稳定。完美预测下限制仍存在：目标从五步增至二十步时成功率由 92% 降至 41%。远距目标下纯想象成功 23%，MPC 反馈规划 30%，想象至目标 47%，邻近专家子目标 76%。在可规划范围内，从 VLA 提出的 8 个动作中筛选可将 16 个任务的成功率从 65% 提升至 77%。

Conclusion: 世界模型的规划能力受"想象轨迹长度与目标距离匹配"这一根本约束限制，而非模型容量或训练长度；有效规划需要更长想象轨迹或更近子目标。在可规划范围内，世界模型可作为即插即用模块提升 VLA 策略性能。

Abstract: World models offer a promising way to help robots understand how the physical world evolves and plan complex behaviours through imagination. Yet existing studies mainly demonstrate what these models can accomplish, leaving unclear when their predictions remain useful for planning and where they fail. We study this question using action-conditioned predictors built on five frozen self-supervised visual backbones: V-JEPA 2, V-JEPA 2.1, VideoMAEv2, VideoPrism, and DINOv2. We use frozen backbones to test representations intended to transfer across environments. We evaluate these models on diverse Meta-World manipulation tasks and real-robot interactions from BridgeData V2. We find that a world model guides action selection reliably only when the goal lies within, or slightly beyond, the trajectory it imagines during planning. With five-step rollouts, the length the predictor was trained on, the world model ranks actions reliably only for targets five to ten control steps ahead, whereas task goals lie 16 to 53 steps away. Neither an 81-fold larger predictor nor longer-rollout training extends this range; the encoder affects both range and closed-loop success, with V-JEPA 2.1 performing most consistently. More fundamentally, the limit persists under perfect prediction: using the real simulator, success falls from 92% to 41% as the target moves from five to twenty steps ahead of a five-step rollout. Planning therefore requires either longer imagined trajectories or closer subgoals. For distant goals, pure imagination succeeds in 23% of episodes, planning with feedback (MPC) raises success to 30%, imagining as far as the goal to 47%, and nearby expert subgoals to 76%. Used within its plannable range, a world model can also improve a vision-language-action (VLA) policy: choosing among eight actions the VLA proposes raises its success from 65% to 77% across 16 different tasks.

</details>


### [171] [ReWAM: Reciprocal World Action Models for Interactive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39245)
*Benshan Ma,Pei Liu,Ruiguo Zhong,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出互惠世界动作模型（ReWAM），以博弈论框架建模自车与其他智能体的相互影响，在NAVSIM上取得最优性能，交互场景提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）将其他智能体视为世界模型的组成部分，而非能根本性塑造自车动作的决策者，导致在密集交互场景中性能不足。论文旨在建模自车与其他智能体之间的互惠影响。

Method: 提出ReWAM，一个博弈论世界动作建模范式，将自车与其他智能体表示为动作相互影响的条件响应者。以Level-k响应层级实例化：角色专属的自车与其他动作DiT通过跨智能体注意力交换紧凑策略token，同时共享未来驾驶世界的表示。将专家动作建模为最佳响应分布的样本，用条件流匹配联合优化整个层级。

Result: 在NAVSIM数据集上相较基线达到SOTA性能，且在交互场景中提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 建模互惠响应为交互感知的世界动作生成提供了更有效的基础，验证了将其他智能体作为决策者建模的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: In interactive scenarios, an autonomous driving system is required to generate ego actions under the influence of other agents' behaviors. Existing World Action Models (WAMs) typically model other agents as components of the world model rather than as decision-makers that fundamentally shape the action of the ego agent, which impairs their performance in dense interaction scenarios. We introduce Reciprocal World Action Models (ReWAM), a game-theoretic world action modeling framework that captures the reciprocal influence between the ego agent and other agents by representing them as conditional responders whose actions are mutually influenced. We instantiate this framework with a Level-$k$ response hierarchy, where role-specific ego and other action DiTs exchange compact strategy tokens through cross-agent attention while remaining grounded in a shared representation of the future driving world. To learn the response policy of the ego agent from demonstrations, we formulate expert actions as samples from the best response distribution and jointly optimize the entire hierarchy using conditional flow matching. Our framework is evaluated on the NAVSIM dataset and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to baselines. The improvement is particularly significant in interactive scenarios, validating that modeling reciprocal responses provides a more effective foundation for interaction-aware world action generation.

</details>


### [172] [Scale and Selection: What Makes Automatic Harness Evolution Work for Visual-Interface Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39304)
*Zhijie Wei,Ferris Tan,Jinghui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明：编码智能体驱动的视觉界面机器人策略其"harness"可被另一个编码智能体（优化者）自动改进；改进效果取决于每次决策所依据的rollout规模，以及是否采用Champion-Challenger选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现成编码智能体直接充当机器人策略（通过截图观察浏览器3D界面、以少量工具摆出虚拟目标夹爪来动作）时，其harness（提示、工具、控制规则）基本决定成败，但迄今一直依靠人工编写。作者希望验证：这一harness能否由另一个编码智能体自动演化改进。

Method: 让一个优化者编码智能体自动迭代改进harness，并开展两组对照实验：（1）固定轮数、将每轮训练rollout数从5增至100，考察rollout规模对晋升决策信噪比、泛化与稳定性的影响；（2）对比"无条件接受每次修订"与加入最基础的Champion-Challenger选择（仅当修订在同一固定评测集上严格优于现任者才晋升）的效果。

Result: 训练rollout从5增至100时，留出成功率由47%升至67%；小训练集过拟合，训练任务上达70%但留出仅54%。无条件接受修订时性能在十轮内持续下滑；采用Champion-Challenger选择后，同一循环在30轮内将留出成功率从51%提升至67%。

Conclusion: 面向视觉界面机器人智能体的harness自动演化是可行的，但其收益取决于每次晋升决策背后的rollout规模，以及优化者修订结果的选择机制；增加批量可提升决策信噪比，而基本的Champion-Challenger保护可防止性能退化并带来持续提升。

Abstract: When an off-the-shelf coding agent is used directly as a robot policy, observing a browser-based 3D interface through screenshots and acting by posing a virtual target gripper through a few tools, the agent's harness, its prompts, tools, and control rules, largely determines success, and until now it has been written by hand. We show that this harness can be improved automatically by another coding agent, the optimizer agent, and report two findings about what makes it work. First, the number of rollouts the optimizer agent sees per round governs whether the evolved harness is trustworthy, generalizes, and improves steadily. A single rollout is a noisy binary outcome, so with few rollouts per round a revision can be promoted on luck; enlarging the batch raises the signal-to-noise ratio of every promotion decision. Holding rounds fixed and growing the training set from 5 to 100 rollouts, held-out success rises from 47% to 67%, while small training sets overfit, reaching 70% on training tasks but only 54% held-out. Second, the optimizer agent must not be given free rein. With every revision it proposes accepted unconditionally, performance drifts downward within ten rounds as ill-judged edits accumulate; adding the most basic safeguard, Champion-Challenger selection that promotes a revision only if it strictly beats the incumbent on the same fixed evaluation set, turns the same loop into one that raises held-out success from 51% to 67% over 30 rounds. Automatic harness evolution for visual-interface robot agents is thus feasible, but its gains hinge on the rollout scale behind each decision and on how the optimizer agent's revisions are selected.

</details>


### [173] [FORTE: Forecasting Occupancy for Spatiotemporal Risk-Aware Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39305)
*Hahjin Lee,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FORTE导航框架，直接利用预测占据栅格的时空演化（时空占据重叠与占据方向性）评估多条拓扑相异路径，配合基于潜在扩散模型的非自回归OGM预测器，实现动态环境下的安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中安全导航需预判未来环境状态以评估时空碰撞风险。现有基于占据栅格地图（OGM）的导航方法预测精度与效率不足，且规划时未充分利用预测OGM中的时序信息。

Method: FORTE利用预测占据的时空演化，从时空占据重叠和占据方向性两个角度评估多条拓扑相异路径并选择合适路径，无需显式目标检测或跟踪；同时构建基于潜在扩散模型的OGM预测器，以非自回归方式生成整个预测时域，并通过时序偏移模块保持时序一致性以支持在线规划。

Result: 预测方面，FORTE的IoU最高提升215.3%，推理速度最高快5.24倍；导航方面，成功率最高提升3.5倍，整体优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: FORTE通过充分利用预测占据栅格的时空演化信息并结合高效非自回归扩散预测，显著提升了动态环境下OGM预测精度与导航成功率及效率。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating future environmental states to account for spatiotemporal risks, specifically when and where collisions may occur. To this end, occupancy grid map (OGM) prediction has been widely adopted as an effective approach. However, existing OGM-based navigation methods often struggle to achieve accurate and efficient forecasting and fail to fully exploit the temporal information in predicted OGMs during planning. To address these challenges, we propose FORTE, a navigation framework that directly exploits the spatiotemporal evolution of predicted occupancy from the perspectives of spatiotemporal occupancy overlap and occupancy directivity. Based on these properties, FORTE evaluates multiple topology-distinct paths and selects the suitable one without explicit object detection or tracking. To support online planning, we formulate a latent diffusion model-based OGM predictor that generates the entire forecast horizon in a non-autoregressive manner while maintaining temporal consistency through temporal shift modules. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FORTE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. For prediction, FORTE achieves up to 215.3% higher IoU and 5.24x faster inference; for navigation, it yields up to a 3.5x higher success rate.

</details>


### [174] [A Biophysically Detailed C. elegans Circuit as a Task-Agnostic Dynamical Core for Visually Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39322)
*Linrui Qian,Jiajia Zhang,Gan He,Bohan Sun,Zhiwei Lin,Qianhao Wang,Zewu Cai,Nianyu Yi,Mengdi Zhao,Kai Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将生物物理精细的秀丽隐杆线虫感觉运动环路（136个多房室神经元）作为视觉运动策略的动力学核心，仅训练轻量任务适配器、固定突触权重，在多任务与真实机械臂上匹配或超越主流基线，并显著提升视觉扰动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略通常针对单一任务、单一本体和单一视觉环境训练，跨条件泛化能力差。神经系统能否通过其演化形成的连接与生物物理特性直接提供感觉运动计算，此前尚无定论。

Method: 把线虫感觉运动环路（136个多房室神经元，具备真实形态与电生理特性）嵌入为视觉运动策略的动力学核心；突触权重保持固定，膜电位自由演化，仅训练轻量的任务专用适配器。通过替换核心为MLP、LSTM、Transformer或储备池网络进行消融，并在MetaWorld多任务及真实机械臂上评测。

Result: 在多类MetaWorld任务上，该核心匹配或超过扩散策略、动作分块Transformer与神经环路策略基线，且在视觉扰动下退化更小；替换为通用网络模型后优势消失；在真实机械臂上，该核心能抵御使基线崩溃的多种视觉扰动。

Conclusion: 视觉鲁棒性可以从生物物理精细的环路动力学中"继承"，而非必须由任务专用控制器学习得到。

Abstract: Robot policies are usually trained for one task, one body and one visual environment, and generalize poorly beyond these conditions. Whether a nervous system can instead supply the sensorimotor computation through its evolved wiring and biophysics remains unresolved. Here we embed a biophysically detailed Caenorhabditis elegans sensorimotor circuit - 136 multicompartment neurons with realistic morphologies and electrophysiological characteristics - as the dynamical core of a visuomotor policy. Only thin task-specific adapters are trained; the core's synaptic weights stay fixed while its membrane voltages evolve freely. Across different MetaWorld tasks the core matches or exceeds diffusion-policy, action-chunking-transformer and neural-circuit-policy baselines, and degrades less under visual perturbations. Replacing the core with generic network models such as MLP, LSTM, transformer or reservoir networks removes the advantage. Furthermore, on a real robotic arm the core withstands diverse visual perturbations that collapse the baselines. Our results suggest that visual robustness can be inherited from biophysically detailed circuit dynamics rather than learned by task-specific controllers.

</details>


### [175] [HiWE: Hierarchical World Knowledge Model with Visual Keypoint Enhancement for Zero-Shot 3D Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39323)
*Guoqing Ma,Mingqi Yuan,Chen Gao,Jiayu Chen,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiWE通过"视觉定位—语言规划"的基于点接口，将交互位置识别、可行运动规划与接触执行统一起来，在14个仿真任务和4个真实机器人任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示生成需要系统同时解决三个问题：交互应在何处发生、如何规划可行运动、以及如何执行所需接触。现有方法往往将视觉定位与语言规划割裂，缺乏统一的中间接口。

Method: 提出基于点（point-based）的接口连接视觉定位与语言规划。PointVLM经指令微调，用点标注、分割衍生样本、机器人观测和VQA数据的混合数据，将任务相关物体关联到图像坐标；深度测量将这些预测提升为语义3D表示。语言规划器3DLLM基于该表示指定末端执行器路点与夹爪指令，混合抓取模块解析局部抓取位姿。

Result: 在14个仿真操作任务和4个物理机器人任务上进行评估，并对视觉训练数据、空间输入和抓取选择进行了消融实验。文中"零样本执行"指不进行任务特定演示训练即部署，视觉模型微调时使用了现有机器人数据。

Conclusion: 本文描述该框架最初的基于点表述，并在引言中说明其与后续GeneralVLA扩展的关系。

Abstract: Robot demonstration generation requires a system to identify where an interaction should occur, plan a feasible motion, and execute the required contact. HiWE connects these decisions through a point-based interface between visual grounding and language-based planning. PointVLM is instruction-tuned to associate task-relevant objects with image coordinates using a mixture of point annotations, segmentation-derived samples, robot observations, and visual question answering data. Depth measurements lift these predictions into a semantic 3D representation. A language planner, 3DLLM, uses this representation to specify end-effector waypoints and gripper commands, while a hybrid grasping module resolves local grasp poses. The evaluation covers 14 simulated manipulation tasks and four physical-robot tasks, together with ablations of the visual training data, spatial inputs, and grasp selection. Here, zero-shot execution refers to deployment without task-specific demonstration training; the visual model uses existing robot data during fine-tuning. This paper describes the original point-based formulation of the framework; its relationship to the subsequent GeneralVLA extension is detailed in the introduction.

</details>


### [176] [MotionWeave: Learning Motion-Centered Future Dynamics for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39324)
*Jingqiu Wang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MotionWeave，一个以运动为中心的未来动力学框架，用于动作块预测，包含AIMG与HRC两个模块，在MetaWorld六个任务上平均成功率达75.3%，比π0提升8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型引入世界模型提供更丰富的动态监督，但显式预测未来图像/视频会引入与控制无关的外观信息；且基于整体未来视觉表征与共享全局动作特征的引导，难以建立动作与局部视觉变化之间逐时间步的对应关系。

Method: 提出MotionWeave框架，含两个模块：Action-Induced Motion Grounder (AIMG)以动作与本体感受表征为条件，构建各时间视界的查询，从当前视觉token中定位与每个未来动作时间步相关的交互区域；Horizon Residual Composer (HRC)提取相邻视界交互表征间的差异，编码为时序运动线索，并通过门控残差注入动作token。训练时利用由未来帧渲染的机械臂掩码构建基于KL的运动定位监督，推理时仅使用当前观测。

Result: 在六个MetaWorld任务上，MotionWeave平均成功率为75.3%，较π0（66.7%）绝对提升8.6%，在持续交互类任务上提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 以运动为中心的局部化未来动力学监督可有效避免控制无关外观干扰，并建立动作与视觉变化的逐时间步对应，从而提升动作块预测性能；推理阶段无需未来帧，具备实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently incorporated world models to provide richer dynamic supervision beyond sparse action labels. However, explicitly predicting future images or videos may include control-irrelevant appearance, while guidance derived from holistic future visual representations and shared global action features may fail to establish timestep-specific correspondence between actions and local visual changes. To address this issue, we propose MotionWeave, a motion-centric future-dynamics framework for action-chunk prediction with two modules: the Action-Induced Motion Grounder (AIMG) and the Horizon Residual Composer (HRC). Specifically, AIMG conditions on action and proprioceptive representations to construct horizon-specific queries that localize interaction regions associated with each future action timestep from current visual tokens. HRC extracts differences between interaction representations at adjacent horizons, encodes them as temporal motion cues, and injects them into action tokens through a gated residual. During training, robot-arm masks rendered from future frames are used to construct KL-based motion-grounding supervision, while inference uses only the current observation. On six MetaWorld tasks, MotionWeave achieves a 75.3% average success rate, an absolute gain of 8.6% over π0 (66.7%), especially on sustained-interaction tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/autu-mn/MotionWeave.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond the Current Scene: Event-Referential Grasping with Active View Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39375)
*Hyunjoon Lee,Haebeom Jung,Eunsung Cha,Daeun Lee,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Jaesung Choe,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零样本机器人抓取系统BeyondSCe，通过事件历史与当前场景几何实现事件指代式抓取，在遮挡条件下结合事件先验进行主动视角选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需响应回溯既往交互事件的请求，此类请求可能以"过去事件中所扮演角色"而非名称或外观指定抓取目标，且目标在受令时可能已不可见，现有方法难以胜任该事件指代与遮挡场景。

Method: BeyondSCe在给定事件历史与当前场景下，识别被请求的物体或部件并定位以用于抓取；若目标被遮挡，则将从历史中恢复的事件先验与当前场景几何相结合，选择可能揭示目标的相机视角。系统完全使用预训练模型，无需额外任务特定训练，仅用单个腕装RGB-D相机。

Result: 真实机器人实验中，初始可见与遮挡目标抓取成功率分别为76%和77%，最强基线各条件下仅为40%和55%；在另外四个重度遮挡场景中，抓取成功率由75%提升至95%，平均视角数由3.35降至2.20（对比给定目标真值3D框的主动感知基线）。

Conclusion: 基于事件先验与场景几何的零样本抓取系统能有效处理事件指代与目标遮挡问题，在成功率与感知效率上均优于强基线，且无需任务特定训练。

Abstract: A robot that observes people interacting with objects should be able to carry out later requests that refer back to those interactions. Such requests may specify a grasp target by the role it played in a past event rather than by its name or appearance. Moreover, the target may no longer be visible when the robot is asked to act. We present BeyondSCe, a zero-shot robotic grasping system for this event-referential setting. Given the event history and the current scene, the system identifies the requested object or part and localizes it for grasping. If the target is occluded, it combines an event prior recovered from the history with current scene geometry to select camera viewpoints likely to reveal the target. The system uses pretrained models without additional task-specific training. In real-robot experiments with a single wrist-mounted RGB-D camera, it achieves grasp success rates of 76% and 77% for initially visible and occluded targets, respectively, compared with 40% and 55% for the strongest baseline in each condition. On four additional scenes with heavy occlusion, it increases grasp success rates from 75% to 95% while reducing the mean number of views from 3.35 to 2.20, compared with an active-perception baseline given the target's ground-truth 3D bounding box.

</details>


### [178] [RoboAssist: Interactive Human-Humanoid Planning for Long-Horizon Surgical Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39384)
*Jingwei Jia,Keyu Zhou,Jiewei Wang,Peisen Xu,Xingyuan Zhou,Liang Wang,Jiming Chen,Gaofeng Li,Jin Wang,Shunlei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAssist：面向长时程手术辅助的人-人形机器人交互式规划框架，融合工作流推理、任务协调与跨层安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术辅助要求人形机器人与不断变化的人类活动协同，并在规划与执行全过程中保持安全，现有方法难以兼顾动态工作流适应与多层安全保障。

Method: 采用非对称双轨表示，将部分可观测的人类流程状态与可执行机器人任务序列分离；在线更新人类流程估计、场景上下文与任务依赖，工作流请求变化时仅重规划受影响的后缀；跨层安全架构包含预防性导航约束、近距离交接的反应式约束及独立的全身运行时监督。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上于多阶段长时程模拟手术辅助场景中完成多阶段任务并适应工作流请求变化；消融显示残差重规划降低计划更新延迟与更新后token消耗；安全实验表明导航、交接与运行时监督提供互补保护。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将在线任务协调与安全约束全流程耦合，有效支持长时程人-人形机器人交互式手术辅助。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical assistance requires humanoid robots to coordinate with evolving human activities while maintaining safety across planning and execution. We present RoboAssist, an agent-based framework for interactive human-humanoid planning that integrates workflow reasoning, task coordination, and cross-layer safety. At its core is an asymmetric dual-track representation that separates partially observed human process states from executable robot task sequences. By updating human-process estimates, scene context, and task dependencies online, RoboAssist revalidates the remaining task sequence and replans only the affected suffix when workflow requests change. A cross-layer safety architecture combines preventive navigation regulation, reactive regulation during close-range handover, and independent whole-body runtime supervision. This design couples online task coordination with safety constraints throughout execution. We demonstrate the framework on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot in long-horizon, multi-stage simulated surgical assistance scenarios encompassing multimodal interaction, instrument handling, medical material transport, navigation, and safe human-robot handover. Experiments show multi-stage task completion and adaptation to workflow-request changes. A targeted full-replanning ablation shows that residual replanning reduces plan-update latency and post-update token usage. Separate safety experiments demonstrate complementary protection across navigation, handover, and runtime supervision. Additional results and demonstrations are available online at https://roboassist.github.io.

</details>


### [179] [UniWAM Technical Report: Unified Mobile Manipulation via Mixed-Stream World-Action Modeling and Manipulation Anchor Pose Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39388)
*Wei Xue,Keliang Liu,Mingzhang Cui,Jinhua Xie,Jinjie Wei,Jianan Hou,Jingcheng Lu,Lintao Wang,Kaixiang Qiu,Yizhou Liu,Xinghai Ye,Jinghang Han,Mingcheng Li,Jie Gu,Shunli Wang,Lihua Zhang,Dingkang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一混合流世界-动作模型UniWAM，通过独立动作编码器与输出头共享骨干，实现导航与操作联合学习，并引入MAP监督及大规模自动构建数据集MAP-Data（150万+回合，7500小时），在MAP-Bench及24项真机任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需先精确导航至可操作位姿，再进行可靠的物体交互。这两个阶段在动作空间与视觉需求上存在差异，难以统一策略学习；同时，采集带显式"可操作位姿"监督的多样化真实导航数据成本高、难扩展。

Method: 提出UniWAM统一混合流世界-动作模型：导航与操作各设独立动作编码器和输出头，共享公共骨干，从而支持在独立采样的导航与操作数据上联合表示学习，并支持单流独立推理与双流批并行推理。进一步提出Manipulation Anchor Pose（MAP）监督，用于确定停止位置与朝向；通过自动化流水线从大规模3D场景构建MAP-Data，提供逐帧目标物体边界框与图像平面MAP坐标作为导航辅助监督，并结合投影的末端执行器轨迹，为从第一视角观测中学习动作提供流特定的图像平面监督。

Result: 基于大规模MAP-Data（超150万回合、7500小时），UniWAM在MAP-Bench上位置误差降低30.1%、朝向误差降低44.0%，优于最强外部基线；在24项真机任务中，MAP导航与移动操作均达领先水平，操作性能具竞争力。

Conclusion: UniWAM通过混合流架构与MAP监督，有效统一了移动操作中的导航与操作学习，并借助可扩展的自动化数据构建缓解监督数据稀缺问题，代码、数据与基准均已开源。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires precise navigation to a manipulation-ready pose followed by reliable object interaction. These two stages differ in action spaces and visual requirements, which complicates unified policy learning. In addition, collecting diverse real-world navigation data with explicit manipulation-ready pose supervision remains costly and difficult to scale. We introduce UniWAM, a unified mixed-stream world-action model with separate action encoders and output heads for navigation and manipulation, sharing a common backbone. This design supports joint representation learning on independently sampled navigation and manipulation data. UniWAM supports independent inference for either stream and batch-parallel inference for both. We further introduce Manipulation Anchor Pose (MAP) supervision for where to stop and how to orient for manipulation. An automated pipeline constructs MAP-Data from large-scale 3D scenes, yielding over 1.5 million episodes and 7,500 hours. MAP-Data provides per-frame target-object bounding boxes and image-plane MAP coordinates as auxiliary navigation supervision. Together with projected end-effector trajectories for manipulation, these prediction targets provide stream-specific image-plane supervision for action learning from egocentric observations. With large-scale MAP-Data, UniWAM outperforms the strongest external baselines on our MAP-Bench by 30.1\% in position error and 44.0\% in heading error. Across 24 real-robot tasks, UniWAM achieves leading results in MAP navigation and mobile manipulation, with competitive manipulation performance. We have released code, data, and benchmark.

</details>


### [180] [IronMind: Scaling Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation via Camera-Space Ego-Centric Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39403)
*Huimin Pan,Yufan Ren,Kunpeng Song,Siyang Wang,Xiwen Zhang,Xiaoyun Hu,Zhuoxu Duan,Hanrui Zheng,Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Sibo Ma,Zhenxuan Fan,Zhongyang Che,Danny Bao,Jiacheng Wei,Jerry Bai,Xiaoyu Yue,Xiaoyang Guo,Chenyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IronMind视觉-语言-动作模型，利用第一人称人类视频和异构机器人数据预训练人形机器人灵巧操作策略，通过相机空间动作表征绕过显式身体重定向，预训练数据规模从250到10000小时验证损失呈对数线性下降，10000小时模型在六项挑战任务上成功率达55.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类视频是可扩展的灵巧操作数据源，但用于人形机器人训练面临两大挑战：（1）具身差距，人手与机器人末端执行器结构不同，且低成本第一人称录制缺乏传统重定向所需的躯干运动学信息；（2）数据质量异构，包括手部姿态跟踪噪声和文本标注弱对齐。

Method: IronMind采用相机空间动作表征（第一人称视频的天然参考空间），并对机器人与人类动作维度进行语义对齐，从而绕过显式身体重定向；使用第一人称人类视频与异构机器人数据进行VLA预训练。

Result: 预训练预算从250到10000小时，验证损失随数据规模近似对数线性下降；后训练后，10000小时模型在六项未见物体、可供性和推理提示的挑战任务上成功率达55.0%，而5000小时以内各预算至多11.7%，无预训练仅5.0%；相同预算下相机空间动作表征优于躯干坐标系基线。

Conclusion: 在大规模人类第一人称数据上以相机空间动作表征进行预训练，可作为人形机器人操作的可扩展基础。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable data source for dexterous manipulation, yet using it to train humanoid robots presents two challenges: (1) an embodiment gap, as human hands differ structurally from robot end-effectors and low-cost egocentric recordings lack the torso kinematics required by conventional retargeting; and (2) heterogeneous data quality, including noisy hand-pose tracking and weakly aligned text annotations. We introduce IronMind, a vision-language-action (VLA) model that uses egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot data to pretrain policies for humanoid dexterous manipulation. To bridge the embodiment gap, IronMind bypasses explicit body-retargeting by using a camera-space action representation, the native reference space of egocentric video, and semantically aligning robot and human action dimensions. Across total pretraining budgets from 250 to 10,000 hours, validation loss decreases approximately log-linearly with data scale. Larger pretraining budgets also improve out-of-distribution real-robot manipulation after post-training: across six challenging tasks with unseen objects, affordances, and reasoning prompts, the 10,000-hour model achieves a 55.0% success rate, compared with at most 11.7% for every pretraining budget up to 5,000 hours and 5.0% without pretraining. At the same pretraining budget, the camera-space action representation also outperforms the torso-frame baseline. Together, these findings support pretraining with a camera-space action representation on large-scale human egocentric data as a scalable foundation for humanoid robot manipulation.

</details>


### [181] [Communication-Free Distributed Multi-Robot Task Allocation under Partial Observations Using Labeled Multi-Bernoulli Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39416)
*Takumi Ito,Akiya Kamimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖局部观测、无需机器人间通信的多机器人任务分配框架，通过LMB滤波估计邻机位置，结合贪心拍卖策略实现去中心化协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多机器人任务分配通常依赖机器人间的显式通信，在通信受限或通信不可靠场景下难以适用。本文旨在实现无需显式通信、仅基于局部观测的 decentralized 协同任务分配。

Method: 将任务定义为到达目标位置；每个机器人利用 Labeled Multi-Bernoulli (LMB) 滤波器估计邻近机器人的位置；基于贪心拍卖策略独立进行任务分配；在执行过程中持续更新状态估计并重新分配任务，从而实现无通信的去中心化协调。

Result: Monte Carlo 仿真表明，该方法在机器人间无通信条件下仍能实现有效的协同任务分配，并对测量杂波与观测不确定性具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 无通信的局部观测式多机器人任务分配框架可行，LMB滤波与贪心拍卖结合可支撑去中心化协同，且在杂波和观测不确定环境下保持稳健。

Abstract: This paper proposes a communication-free multi-robot task allocation framework based solely on local observations. In this study, tasks are defined as reaching target locations. Each robot estimates the positions of neighboring robots using a Labeled Multi-Bernoulli (LMB) filter and independently assigns tasks through a greedy auction-based strategy. By continuously updating state estimates and reallocating tasks during execution, the proposed method enables decentralized coordination without explicit communication. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that the proposed method enables effective cooperative task allocation without inter-robot communication while remaining robust to measurement clutter and observation uncertainty.

</details>


### [182] [LIBERO-Agent: Evaluating General-Purpose Agents for Direct Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39507)
*Zijie Diao,Yitong Chen,Sicheng Xie,Tianyi Lu,Wujian Peng,Guojin Zhong,Houze Xu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIBERO-Agent，一个面向通用智能体的机器人操作交互式基准，包含 200 个任务与 30 个任务的主测试套件，用于检验通用智能体的规划与工具使用能力能否迁移到具身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体已具备规划、工具使用与基于反馈的行为修正能力，但这些能力能否从数字环境迁移到具身操作任务仍不明确，现有基准多要求提交任务级控制程序或依赖高层技能接口，难以直接考察智能体的原生交互能力。

Method: 提出 LIBERO-Agent：智能体在交互式机器人环境中自主选择观测、使用自有工具处理观测，并直接下达原生动作指令；统一 200 个任务的交互框架，并构建 30 个任务的主测试套件，将感知、短时程执行与长时程组合三类能力分离评估。

Result: 结果显示出显著的可靠性差距：智能体在感知任务和简单短时程任务上表现良好，但在困难短时程与长时程任务上性能大幅下降；更丰富的观测改善短时程操作，示范数据的收益则取决于智能体与数据格式。GPT-6 Astra 综合性能最强。

Conclusion: GPT-6 Astra 的主要优势来自机制交互，尤其是需要持续物理接触的场景；其剩余失败主要源于跨阶段干扰与几何误差，表明通用智能体向具身操作的迁移仍存在明显可靠性瓶颈。

Abstract: General-purpose agents can plan, use tools, and revise their behavior from feedback, but it remains unclear whether these capabilities transfer from digital environments to embodied manipulation. To investigate this question, we introduce LIBERO-Agent, an agent-native benchmark for evaluating these agents in robot manipulation tasks. Rather than asking agents to submit task-level Python control programs or operate through high-level robot skills, LIBERO-Agent provides an interactive robotic environment where agents can select which observations to inspect, process them with their own tools, and issue native action commands. LIBERO-Agent integrates 200 tasks into a common interaction framework and provides a 30-task primary suite that separates perception, short-horizon execution, and long-horizon composition. Results reveal a pronounced reliability gap: while agents perform well on perception and easy short-horizon tasks, their performance degrades substantially on hard short-horizon and long-horizon tasks. Richer observations improve short-horizon manipulation, while demonstration benefits depend on the agent and format. Among these agents, GPT-6 Astra achieves the strongest overall performance. Further analysis shows its major advantage lies in mechanism interaction, especially when sustained physical contact is needed, while its remaining failures stem from cross-stage interference and geometric errors.

</details>


### [183] [Discrete Forcing: Infusing Discrete Guidance into Continuous Denoising for Few-Step Action Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39526)
*Jingbo Wang,Wenxuan Song,Wenhao Yu,Han Zhao,Xi Wang,Jiayi Chen,Donglin Wang,Yan Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Discrete Forcing 框架，通过"离散结构预测+连续细节精修"的显式粗到细流程生成 VLA 动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型中，离散动作 token 具备紧凑的结构表征但精度不足，连续动作 token 精度高却常需多步去噪。二者难以兼得粗粒度的动作结构与细粒度的动作细节。

Method: 基于 flow-matching 的 Discrete Forcing 框架：（1）先预测离散动作 token 建立粗动作结构；（2）用其引导连续动作的精修。离散与连续分支共享同一 diffusion transformer 主干并配置专用分支，参数量与常规单分支模型相当，且每个分支仅需一次前向。

Result: 在多个 benchmark 上性能优于参数匹配的连续动作专家，推理更快；随模型容量增大性能持续提升；真实世界实验在高精度与动态操作任务上取得改进。

Conclusion: Discrete Forcing 将离散结构先验与连续精修统一于单主干框架，在保持参数效率与单步前向的前提下，实现了性能与推理速度的双重收益，并在真机高精度、动态操作中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Efficient action generation in vision-language-action (VLA) models requires capturing both coarse action structure and fine-grained details. Discrete action tokens provide compact structural representations but sacrifice precision, while continuous action tokens offer high precision but often require multiple denoising steps. We introduce Discrete Forcing, a flow-matching framework that combines these representations through an explicit coarse-to-fine generation process. It first predicts discrete action tokens to establish a coarse action structure, then uses them to guide continuous action refinement. The discrete and continuous components share a common diffusion transformer backbone with specialized branches, maintaining a parameter count comparable to a conventional single-branch model while requiring only one forward pass per branch. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate improved performance and faster inference over a parameter-matched continuous action expert, with consistent performance gains as model capacity increases. Real-world experiments further demonstrate improvements on high-precision and dynamic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [184] [General Performance Guarantee for Human Torque Estimation-Based Task-Agnostic Assistive Exoskeleton Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39558)
*Duy Hoang,Bastien Berret,Olivier Bruneau,Laurent Fribourg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对外骨骼机器人中人体力矩估计误差导致辅助与意图不匹配的问题，将"匹配辅助"形式化定义为机器人对人体运动作出正贡献的情景，并提出理论框架设计期望交互力矩，保证匹配辅助概率下界，且该保证覆盖整个力矩分布（含训练任务外的未见数据）。在ABLE上肢外骨骼上多任务验证，结果验证了理论保证，实现跨任务通用性能，兼顾运动平滑性并降低人体体力消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的人体力矩估计对实现任务无关的机器人外骨骼控制至关重要。然而估计误差会造成机器人辅助与人体意图不匹配，降低可控性与任务表现。作者希望解决该匹配问题，并获得可靠性与泛化性保障。

Method: 形式化定义"匹配辅助"为机器人对人体运动产生正贡献的情景；据此构建理论框架，设计机器人的期望交互力矩，从而给出匹配辅助概率的下界；该保证在整个力矩分布上成立，包括训练任务之外的未见数据。方法在ABLE上肢外骨骼上实现并进行多任务评估。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的理论保证；该策略在多个任务上取得有效的通用性能，保证运动平滑的同时降低人体体力消耗。

Conclusion: 通过形式化匹配辅助并设计带概率下界保证的期望交互力矩，可在整个力矩分布（含未见数据）上提供可靠性，实现跨任务的通用外骨骼控制，兼顾平滑性与省力效果。

Abstract: Accurate human torque estimation is crucial for enabling task-agnostic control in robotic exoskeleton systems. However, estimation errors may cause mismatches between the robot assistance and the human intention, degrading controllability and task performance. In this paper, we address this issue by formally defining matched assistance as scenarios in which the robot positively contributes to human movement. Based on this definition, we develop a theoretical framework to design the robot's desired interaction torque that guarantees a lower bound on the matched assistance probability. Importantly, the proposed guarantee holds over the entire torque distribution, including unseen data beyond the training tasks. This provides our method with strong reliability and generalization, both of which are critical for effective exoskeleton control. The proposed strategy is implemented on the ABLE upper-limb exoskeleton and evaluated in a multi-task setup. Experimental results validate the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that the proposed strategy achieves effective general performance across several tasks, guaranteeing movement smoothness while reducing human physical effort.

</details>


### [185] [Sparse Planner: A Hybrid Planner for Efficient Sampling via a Conditional Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39570)
*Wenguang Xu,Giovanni Lucente,Karem Mohamed,Richard Membarth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏规划器（SP），利用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）学习场景上下文与有效轨迹参数间的条件关系，从而在参数空间直接生成低成本样本，以更低采样密度实现高质量轨迹。实验表明，SP仅用八分之一采样密度即取得优于FISS+的轨迹成本，并在障碍密集场景中具备更好的距离保持能力和更稳定、更低的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹规划是自动驾驶系统的核心组件，其实时性与解质量直接影响安全性和可靠性。基于采样的运动规划（SBMP）虽能通过参数空间采样逼近近优解，但获得高质量轨迹通常需要密集采样，导致计算开销大，且在复杂交通场景中运行时间波动显著。因此，如何在保证解质量的前提下降低采样密度、提升计算效率并稳定运行时间，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出稀疏规划器（SP）：使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）学习场景上下文与有效轨迹参数之间的条件关系；通过对高质量采样分布的结构建模，在参数空间中直接生成具有成本效益的样本，从而在保持解质量的同时显著降低所需采样密度。

Result: SP在仅使用八分之一采样密度的情况下，轨迹成本低于当前最先进的FISS+规划器；在障碍密集场景中展现出更好的距离保持能力；同时运行时间更低且更稳定，表明其计算效率提升、运行时间行为更可预测。

Conclusion: 通过CVAE学习场景上下文与有效轨迹参数的映射，SP能够在参数空间直接采样高性价比样本，以更低采样密度获得优于FISS+的轨迹质量，并改善障碍密集场景的距离保持与运行时间的稳定性，验证了学习引导采样在提升SBMP效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning is a core component of autonomous driving systems, where real-time performance and solution quality directly affect safety and reliability. Sample-Based Motion Planning (SBMP) is widely adopted for its ability to approximate near-optimal solutions through parameter space sampling. However, achieving high-quality trajectories typically requires dense sampling, leading to substantial computational overhead and significant runtime variability in complex traffic scenarios.
  To address this limitation, we propose a Sparse Planner (SP) that improves sampling efficiency by learning the conditional relationship between scene context and effective trajectory parameters using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). By modeling the structure of high-quality sampling distributions, SP directly generates cost-effective samples in the parameter space, significantly reducing the required sampling density while preserving solution quality.
  Experimental results show that SP achieves lower trajectory cost than the state-of-the-art FISS+ planner while using only one-eighth of the sampling density. In addition, SP demonstrates improved distance-keeping capability in obstacle-rich scenarios and maintains reduced and more stable runtime characteristics, indicating enhanced computational efficiency and predictable runtime behavior.

</details>


### [186] [ECHO-G: Embodied Co-speech Humanoid mOtion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39575)
*Yizhao Li,Pusen Gao,Ming Wang,Shaojie Shen,Shuo Yang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ECHO-G 框架，以语音音频与带时间戳文本为联合条件，直接在机器人运动空间生成全身共语动作，并配套数据集与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身共语动作生成需同时协调语音韵律、语言内容与具身特定运动；既有做法多先生成人类动作再重定向，难以保证机器空间下的运动质量与一致性。

Method: 核心为语音锚定的扩散 Transformer（SGDiT），将帧对齐声学特征与词元级语言上下文结合并保持各自粒度；采用 rectified flow matching 训练，直接在机器人空间建模一对多的语句-动作关系。同时构建基于 BEAT2 的音频-文本-机器人数据集及涵盖共语特性、机器人运动质量与运行时效率的评测基准。

Result: 对比评测表明，直接在机器人空间生成优于所比较的人类动作生成加重定向流程；模态消融显示音频-文本联合条件的增益；并在实体人形机器人上完成部署验证。

Conclusion: 联合音频-文本条件、面向机器人空间的直接生成在共语动作任务上更有效；额外的视频评分研究同样支持联合条件方案。数据集与训练、推理、评测代码已通过项目主页公开。

Abstract: Generating full-body co-speech motion for humanoid robots requires coordinating speech prosody, linguistic content, and embodiment-specific motion. To this end, we present ECHO-G, a framework jointly conditioned on speech audio and timed transcripts. Its Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer (SGDiT) combines frame-aligned acoustic features with token-level linguistic context, preserving their distinct granularities. Trained with rectified flow matching, it models one-to-many utterance-motion relationships directly in robot space. To support training and evaluation, we introduce a BEAT2-derived audio-text-robot dataset and a benchmark covering co-speech characteristics, robot-motion quality, and runtime efficiency. Comparative evaluation supports direct robot-space generation over the evaluated human-motion generation and retargeting pipelines, while modality ablations highlight the benefits of joint audio-text conditioning. We further demonstrate deployment on a physical humanoid robot. A complementary video-rating study also favors joint conditioning over the alternatives. The dataset and training, inference, and evaluation code are available through our project page.

</details>


### [187] [Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39594)
*Zihan Ye,Jiayi Liu,Puze Liu,Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NEUPRO，一种神经符号谓词学习方法，通过可微推理器从人类指定的符号安全规则中学习可复用的安全表征，并发布首个可解释机器人安全规约的真实机器人基准数据集REASON。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在日常环境中部署日益广泛，其安全性成为核心挑战。现有方法将安全需求编码为不透明的数学/逻辑公式或稠密代价函数，虽在特定任务上有效，但难以解释、与单个任务紧耦合，且无法说明机器人动作为何被视为安全或不安全。

Method: 提出NEUPRO，利用可微推理器，允许从业者以透明符号规则表达任务相关安全需求，同时让梯度通过规则传播至特征提取器，将原始观测映射为安全相关概念。学习到的特征提取器被（软性地）锚定于人类可理解的语义中，支持透明的约束评估并可跨任务迁移。

Result: 在REASON上的实验表明，NEUPRO学习到的安全关键特征能够跨任务泛化，缓解了传统黑盒代价公式的可解释性局限，并能提供安全违规的显式解释。

Conclusion: NEUPRO将可解释性与可微性结合，超越了不透明的代价设计，走向可复用的安全推理；同时发布的REASON是首个面向可解释机器人安全规约的真实机器人基准数据集。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in everyday environments, ensuring their safety has become a central challenge. Existing methods often encode safety requirements as opaque mathematical/logical formulations or dense cost functions. While effective in specific tasks, they remain difficult to interpret, tightly coupled to individual tasks, and offer limited insight into why a robot action is considered safe or unsafe. To address this limitation, we propose ``Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control'' (NEUPRO), which leverages a differentiable reasoner that can learn reusable safety representations from human-specified safety knowledge. NEUPRO allows practitioners to express task-related safety requirements as transparent symbolic rules, while enabling gradients to propagate through these rules to a feature extractor that maps raw observations to safety-relevant concepts. As a result, the learned feature extractor is (softly) grounded in human-understandable semantics, supports transparent constraint evaluation, and is transferable across tasks. By coupling interpretability with differentiability, NEUPRO moves beyond opaque cost design toward reusable safety reasoning. To evaluate NEUPRO's capability, we collect and release REASON, the first real robot benchmark dataset for interpretable robot safety specification. Experiments on REASON show that NEUPRO learns safety-critical features that generalize across tasks, mitigate the interpretability limitations of conventional black-box cost formulations, and provide explicit explanations of safety violation.

</details>


### [188] [MVP-SLAM: Multi-Camera Visual-Inertial Floorplan-Prior SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39596)
*Asier Bikandi-Noya,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Muhammad Shaheer,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-SLAM：一种仅用双鱼眼相机的在线视觉惯性SLAM，通过将地图中检测到的墙体与平面图匹配实现漂移感知校正，在Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026室内施工现场定位任务中排名第2（0.29 m RMSE），SLAM任务排名第5（0.24 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内施工现场环境对视觉SLAM极具挑战：光照变化大、结构重复且低纹理，导致长轨迹漂移加剧。尽管实际建筑与设计图存在差异，平面图仍能提供度量参考，可用于建筑内定位与漂移校正。现有方法多在离线阶段用平面图纠正轨迹，在线方法则通常依赖深度传感器。

Method: 提出MVP-SLAM，基于两个对向鱼眼相机的在线视觉惯性SLAM系统。仅依赖相机数据，通过漂移感知策略将地图中检测到的墙体与平面图进行匹配；随后通过多阶段集成，将每个匹配对逐步转化为持久性校正，使轨迹在构建过程中保持校正并在平面图内实现定位。

Result: 在Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026多层建筑施工现场数据集上验证：定位任务22支队伍中排名第2（平均RMSE 0.29 m），SLAM任务62支队伍中排名第5（0.24 m）；在两个任务中均为同时满足在线运行、集成平面图并在其中定位的队伍中排名第一。

Conclusion: MVP-SLAM证明仅依靠对向鱼眼相机的在线视觉惯性SLAM，结合平面图墙体匹配，可在复杂室内施工现场实现有效的漂移校正与建筑内定位，无需深度传感器，且在权威基准上表现领先。

Abstract: Indoor building construction sites are demanding environments for visual SLAM, where variable lighting and repetitive, low-textured structures make the system drift over long trajectories, though structural elements such as walls remain distinguishable despite these conditions. These buildings are constructed according to their as-planned floor plans, available from the design phase, and although the actual as-built site can differ from this design, floor plans still provide a metric reference, both to localize the system in the building and to correct drift. Existing methods often use the floor plan to correct an already-built trajectory offline, and those that instead correct it online typically rely on depth sensors. We instead present MVP-SLAM, an online visual-inertial SLAM on two opposite-facing fisheye cameras that corrects drift from cameras alone by matching walls detected in its map to the floor plan, through a drift-aware policy. A multi-stage integration then turns each matched pair incrementally into a persistent correction, so the trajectory stays corrected and localized within the floor plan as it is built. MVP-SLAM was validated on the multi-floor construction sites of the Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026, ranking 2nd of 22 teams in the Localization task (0.29 m mean RMSE) and 5th of 62 teams in the SLAM task (0.24 m), the top-ranked one in both tasks among those that operate online, integrate the floor plan, and localize within it.

</details>


### [189] [Text-to-3D Policy: Fine-Grained Language-Behavior Alignment for Unseen Specification Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39599)
*Xinhao Yang,Wenhao Wu,Ning Lv,Yanshen Ding,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出T3DP，一个面向细粒度语言-行为对齐的文本到3D策略框架，通过建立语言单元与行为片段间的双向token级对应关系，解决未见细粒度行为规范的泛化问题，在多个仿真与真实机器人任务上显著超越全局对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D策略难以遵循演示未覆盖的、未见过的细粒度行为规范（如目标位置、位移或关节状态等语言指定的行为变化）。预训练语言表征与传统的全局行为-语言对齐只能捕捉粗粒度任务语义，却常常混淆需要不同行为的相近规范，导致表示空间中相近规范坍缩。

Method: 提出T3DP框架。不做全局压缩式嵌入，而是保留指令与演示的局部结构，建立语言元素与行为片段之间的双向token级对应关系，将细微语言变化直接接地到受影响的行为组件上。由此生成的规范敏感语言表征用于条件化一个基于点云的3D扩散策略，在不改动底层策略架构的前提下实现更精确的控制。

Result: 在Meta-World、ManiSkill和RoboTwin上，T3DP相较全局语言-行为对齐在留出规范成功率上平均提升+11.0至14.2个百分点，且在全部15个任务族上均有增益；在真实机器人任务上，平均成功率从47.5%提升至65.0%（+17.5个百分点）。表征与动作探针分析显示，细粒度对齐更好地保留了规范几何与动作相关变化。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言-行为对齐能将局部行为接地与下游控制有效关联，显著提升对未见行为规范的泛化能力，且无需修改策略架构即可增强控制精度。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies provide a strong foundation for spatially precise manipulation, yet current text-to-3D policies struggle to follow unseen fine-grained behavioral specifications beyond those covered by demonstrations. We study this challenge as unseen specification generalization, where language specifies behaviorally significant variations, such as target position, displacement, or articulated state, that are absent from policy training. We find that pretrained language representations and conventional global behavior-language alignment capture coarse task semantics but often blur nearby specifications that require distinct behaviors. We introduce T3DP, a Text-to-3D Policy framework for fine-grained language-behavior alignment. Rather than compressing each instruction and demonstration into a single global embedding, T3DP preserves their local structures and establishes bidirectional token-level correspondence between linguistic elements and behavioral segments. This directly grounds subtle linguistic variations in the behavior components they affect, preventing closely related specifications from collapsing in the representation space. The resulting specification-sensitive language representation conditions a point-cloud-based 3D diffusion policy, enabling more precise control over unseen behavioral specifications without modifying the underlying policy architecture. Across Meta-World, ManiSkill, and RoboTwin, T3DP improves average held-out-specification success over global language-behavior alignment by +11.0-14.2 points, with gains on all 15 task families; on real-robot tasks, it further raises average success from 47.5% to 65.0% (+17.5 points). Representation and action-probe analyses show that fine-grained alignment better preserves specification geometry and action-relevant variation, linking local behavior grounding to downstream control.

</details>


### [190] [Towards Agile Vision-Based Multi-UAV Flight: Revisiting State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39611)
*Michal Pliska,Matouš Vrba,Ondřej Víta,Martin Jiroušek,Viktor Walter,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合倾角测量的位姿感知估计方法，消除仅位置估计的结构性延迟，将速度/加速度误差降低40%/57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷多无人机飞行需低延迟机载估计邻机运动状态以支持避碰与协同。多数视觉方法仅依赖位置测量，通过位移间接推断速度与加速度，这会引入固定结构性延迟，限制可达敏捷性。

Method: 引入先进视觉检测器提供的倾角测量（反映共面多旋翼推力方向），基准对比4种仅位置估计器与5种位姿感知估计器，其中包括新提出的线性推力约束卡尔曼滤波（KF）公式；在2个真实数据集和1个高保真照片级仿真数据集上，覆盖3–21 m/s²的敏捷度进行评测。

Result: 位姿感知估计在三数据集上平均降低速度误差40%、加速度误差57%，所提KF公式优于其他估计器。仅位置滤波器在加速度阶跃响应中呈现约300 ms恒定延迟且与敏捷度无关；倾角约束估计器接近相机帧率决定的物理响应极限，因在位移累积前即观测到推力方向变化。闭环leader-follower NMPC仿真中，仅位置估计无法维持跟随者稳定悬停，而所提估计器可跟踪超过2g的横向机动。

Conclusion: 融合倾角（推力方向）测量可打破仅位置视觉估计的结构性延迟瓶颈，显著提升多无人机协同中的状态估计精度与闭环敏捷跟踪能力。

Abstract: Agile multi-UAV flight requires accurate and low-latency onboard estimation of the kinematic states of neighboring UAVs for collision avoidance, motion coordination, etc. Most vision-based approaches rely on position-only measurements, inferring velocity and acceleration indirectly from displacement. We show that this introduces a fixed structural delay in the estimation of higher-order states, which limits the achievable agility. To address this, we propose to integrate tilt measurements, provided by a state-of-the-art visual detector, which inform about the thrust direction of co-planar multirotor UAVs. We benchmark four position-only and five pose-aware estimators, including a novel formulation of a linear thrust-constraining Kalman filter, on two real-world and one high-fidelity photorealistic simulated dataset over different levels of agility (3-21 m/s^2). In our setup, pose-aware estimation consistently reduces the average velocity and acceleration estimation errors by 40% and 57% across the three datasets with the proposed KF formulation outperforming the other estimators. Position-only filters exhibit a constant ~300 ms delay in acceleration step response independent of agility, whereas the tilt-constrained estimators operate near the physical response limit given by the camera frame-rate by observing the change in thrust direction before the displacement accumulates. In a closed-loop leader-follower simulated experiment with NMPC control, position-only estimation of the leader's state fails to facilitate stable hovering of the follower, while the proposed estimator enables tracking of lateral maneuvers exceeding 2g of acceleration.

</details>


### [191] [TO-mdiSPAs: Topology Optimization of multi-directional Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39618)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拓扑优化的系统化方法，用于设计多向软体气动执行器（SPA），通过三场鲁棒性建模、Darcy排水载荷模型及min-max优化实现非常规高性能几何构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动执行器在气压载荷下产生弯曲变形，多向/全向SPA可借助多自由度在三维空间任意方向运动。然而，如何系统化地获得兼顾多向变形能力与制造鲁棒性的优化构型，仍缺乏有效设计方法，因此本文引入拓扑优化来解决该设计问题。

Method: 采用拓扑优化（TO）构建系统化设计流程。为实现制造鲁棒性，引入考虑blueprint、dilated与eroded三种设计的三场TO公式；采用含排水项的Darcy定律描述气压载荷的设计依赖性；以SPA单元的目标输出变形为基础建立min-max优化问题，并使用移动渐近线法（MMA）求解。

Result: 优化得到一种高性能、非常规的几何设计；数值仿真表明，优化后的SPA能够成功实现多样化的多向运动。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于三场鲁棒性TO与Darcy载荷建模的min-max优化框架，可有效获得多向SPA的优化构型，并验证其多向运动的可行性与性能。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators (SPAs) are highly promising and have been extensively explored within the field of soft robotics. Under pneumatic pressure loads, an SPA undergoes bending deformation to perform specific tasks. A multi- or omni-directional SPA can bend and move in any direction within a 3D space, leveraging its multiple degrees of freedom to realize various mechanical functions. This work presents a systematic methodology using topology optimization (TO) to achieve an optimized design for a multi-directional SPAs. To ensure manufacturing robustness, a three-field TO formulation considering blueprint, dilated, and eroded designs is implemented. Additionally, Darcy's law, incorporating a drainage term, is used to model the design-dependent nature of the pneumatic loading. A min-max optimization problem is formulated based on the target output deformations of the SPA unit and solved using the Method of Moving Asymptotes. The optimization yields a high-performing, unconventional geometric design. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate that the optimized SPA successfully achieves versatile multi-directional movements.

</details>


### [192] [Prediction is Better than Detection: Traffic Congestion Control using Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39637)
*Samira Hayat,Christian Raffelsberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过多智能体仿真研究无人机车队规模对交通拥堵检测与预测性能的影响，发现在车队规模接近监测路口数时性能趋于饱和；且基于预测（而非检测）拥堵来调整信号灯可将拥堵时长缩减效果翻倍，表明机载预测精度而非感知覆盖是进一步优化的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是部署移动机器人车队进行持续监测时，任务性能如何随车队规模扩展，以及当感知驱动下游行动（而非仅观测）时这种扩展规律是否依然成立。作者以无人机在仿真路网中检测和预测交通拥堵、其报告驱动自适应信号控制器闭环为例来研究该问题。

Method: 构建多智能体仿真：车辆遵循Nagel-Schreckenberg元胞自动机动力学；无人机采用轮询策略巡逻路口；通过扫描车队规模、交通水平和网络规模，评估检测率、检测延迟和预测率。

Result: 性能在车队规模约等于所监测路口数量时出现平台期；据此提出通用的车队供给规则。更重要的是，基于预测（而非检测）的拥堵进行信号调整，可使拥堵时长缩减效果约翻倍，说明机载预测在感知-行动管线中的价值可超过增加更多机器人。

Conclusion: 预测精度而非感知覆盖成为进一步提升的约束瓶颈，未来工作更应关注机载推理（onboard inference）而非扩大车队规模。

Abstract: A central question in deploying teams of mobile robots for persistent monitoring is how task performance scales with fleet size, and whether this scaling holds once sensing drives downstream action rather than mere observation. We study this question for a team of drones performing traffic-jam detection and prediction in a simulated road network, whose reports drive an adaptive traffic-signal controller in closed loop. We build a multi-agent simulation, with vehicles following Nagel-Schreckenberg cellular-automaton dynamics and drones patrolling junctions via a round-robin policy, and sweep fleet size, traffic level, and network size to evaluate detection rate, detection delay, and prediction rate. We show how performance plateaus for fleet size approximating the number of junctions being monitored, and offer a general fleet-provisioning rule for persistent-monitoring deployments. More significantly, adapting the signal on a predicted jam, rather than a detected one, roughly doubles the resulting reduction in jam duration, showing that the value of onboard prediction in a sensing-to-action pipeline can exceed the value of adding more robots. Prediction accuracy, not sensing coverage, is now the binding constraint on further improvement, pointing to onboard inference, not fleet size, as the more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [193] [Making Waves: A Membrane-Coupled Delta Array for Manipulating Objects Below the Actuator Spacing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39652)
*Bailey Dacre,Andrés Faíña,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过将8x8三自由度delta机器人阵列的末端执行器用可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触点变为连续表面，突破了执行器间距对可操纵物体尺寸的下限，并利用位移场原语与DCT系数策略实现多物体并行操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机械手系统依赖多个执行器协同运动来操纵物体，但物体必须同时被若干执行器支撑，因此执行器中心间距对可操纵物体的最小尺寸施加了硬性下界，限制了小尺寸物体的操作能力。

Method: 将8x8个三自由度delta机器人的末端执行器与可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触点转化为连续表面；将阵列视为该表面上的位移场，研究局部准静态场与循环场作为操作原语，可全局施加或围绕每个被跟踪物体局部限制以并行独立操作多个物体；随后训练一个作用于低阶离散余弦变换（DCT）系数的策略。

Result: 在相同动作维度下，命令19个delta的邻域相比命令整个阵列使放置误差减半；策略无需自适应即可迁移到硬件，成功率为76%；平台可在单表面上操纵15mm至90mm的物体，达到六倍尺寸范围，跨越执行器间距43.3mm的两侧。

Conclusion: 通过用可拉伸织物耦合分布式执行器阵列形成连续表面，并结合位移场操作原语与低阶DCT系数策略，成功消除了执行器间距对物体尺寸的下界，实现了跨间距两侧的宽尺寸范围操作与多物体并行操作，且策略可零自适应迁移至硬件。

Abstract: Distributed manipulator systems manipulate objects through the coordinated motion of many actuators. However, an object must be supported by several actuators at once, so the centre-to-centre actuator spacing imposes a hard lower bound on manipulable object size. We remove this bound by coupling the end-effectors of an 8 x 8 array of three degrees-of-freedom delta robots with a stretchable fabric, turning 64 discrete contacts into a continuous surface capable of manipulating objects smaller than the actuator spacing. Viewing the array as a displacement field over that surface, we investigate local quasi-static and cyclic fields as manipulation primitives. These primitives can be applied globally across the array or locally confined around each tracked object to independently manipulate several objects in parallel. We then train a policy acting on low-order discrete cosine transform coefficients: at equal action dimension, commanding a nineteen-delta neighbourhood halves the placement error of commanding the whole array. The policy transfers to hardware without adaptation at 76% success. The platform manipulates objects from 15mm-90mm, a six-fold range spanning both sides of the actuator spacing, 43.3mm, on a single surface.

</details>


### [194] [From Local Whole-Body VLA Behaviors to Scene-Scale Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39670)
*Weixiang Guo,Rui Jin,Haotian Jin,Xinhang Xu,Ruiyang Liu,Haoran Zhao,Yi Wang,Weiqi Gai,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向关节式无人机操作臂（UAM）场景级任务的统一框架，融合合成策略训练与实机执行：通过场景可重构流水线生成无实体示教的VLA训练数据，用MPAR解决异步动作块与执行进度的错位问题，并借助关系场景图与拓扑引导迁移实现跨站点行为组合。仿真实测局部技能成功率65.0%，500ms延迟下MPAR将接管相位误差中位数降低0.212s，多站点任务完成率42.0%，并在实体UAM上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型扩展到场景级空中操作面临三大障碍：获取全身演示成本高昂；系统延迟导致动作与状态错位；以及跨场地的行为难以组合复用。

Method: 1）场景可重构流水线：合成任务条件化、满足运动动力学可行性的轨迹及同步多视角观测，无需实体平台演示即可训练VLA；2）测量进度对齐实现（MPAR）：将异步返回的动作块与实测执行进度对齐，并实现为连续、动态可行的轨迹；3）关系场景图：把语言目标落地到物体实例与可行交互区域；4）拓扑引导迁移：跨站点连接局部行为。

Result: 仿真中，在oracle目标与可行交接条件下，局部VLA技能成功39/60（65.0%）；在增加500ms延迟、含或不含瞬时指令更新停顿的条件下，MPAR相比标称时间对齐将接管相位误差中位数降低0.212s；完整系统在仿真中完成21/50多站点任务（42.0%），并在实体关节式UAM上进一步验证。

Conclusion: 该统一框架无需实体全身演示即可训练VLA策略，并通过MPAR对齐与场景图/拓扑迁移实现了延迟鲁棒、可跨站点组合的场景级空中操作，仿真与实体实验共同验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable task-conditioned interaction, but extending them to scene-scale aerial manipulation remains challenging due to costly whole-body demonstrations, latency-induced action-state misalignment, and cross-site behavior composition. We present a unified framework for synthetic policy training and scene-scale execution on articulated uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs). A scene-reconfigurable pipeline synthesizes task-conditioned, kinodynamically feasible trajectories and synchronized multiview observations for VLA training without physical-platform demonstrations. Measured-progress-aligned realization (MPAR) aligns asynchronously returned action chunks with measured execution progress and realizes them as continuous, dynamically feasible trajectories.
