Second, we derive a unified PMP connecting the Itô and rough PMPs, using Itô-Stratonovich conversion formulas and duality identities between the forward tangent and backward adjoint SDEs. As a first application, we rederive the adjoint matching method for fine-tuning generative models. As a second application, we propose an indirect shooting method for a class of feedback problems. Overall, these results give a new conditional bridge connecting two popular frameworks for stochastic optimal control.

</details>


### [228] [TACIT: Optimization Models that Learn from Their Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38434)
*Maxime Bouscary,Marco Molinaro,Sirui Li,Saurabh Amin,Ishai Menache,Konstantina Mellou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 TACIT 框架，利用 LLM 的推理与先验知识结合优化的形式化能力，通过"自上而下"结构修复与"自下而上"割平面解释两条互补路径，自动修复历史数据中隐含的优化模型错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实优化问题中许多目标与约束存在于专家的隐性知识里，难以形式化，导致模型常出现目标校准偏差、约束缺失或决策变量遗漏，使解无法反映真实运营。传统逆优化与约束学习方法在稀疏数据上易过拟合，且产生形式复杂的模型。

Method: 利用历史解与后续用户覆盖数据自动修复错配建模。两条互补范式：自上而下由 LLM 提出结构性修复（新增约束与变量），其数值参数经优化校准与验证；自下而上由优化从观测决策中推断割平面，再由 LLM 将其上下文化为可解释、可泛化的建模约束。

Result: 在覆盖九类优化问题、含多个真实应用场景的 38 个错配情景上评估，TACIT 可修复 78.9%，最强基线为 60.5%。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 的推理先验与优化的形式化基础相结合，能有效修复隐性知识导致的模型错配，并在多类问题上优于既有基线方法。

Abstract: Real-world optimization problems are difficult to model accurately because many objectives and constraints reside in domain experts' tacit knowledge, making them hard to formalize. As a result, optimization models often contain miscalibrated objectives, missing constraints, or omitted decision variables, leading to solutions that fail to reflect operational realities. We address this challenge by automatically repairing misspecified formulations using historical data consisting of past solutions and subsequent user overrides. Traditional approaches such as inverse optimization and constraint learning tend to overfit sparse data and produce complex formulations. Our central idea is to combine the reasoning capabilities and prior knowledge of LLMs with the formal grounding provided by optimization. We realize this idea through two complementary paradigms. Top-down, an LLM proposes structural repairs, including new constraints and variables, whose numerical parameters are calibrated and validated through optimization. Bottom-up, optimization infers cuts from observed decisions, which the LLM contextualizes into interpretable, generalizable modeling constraints. We evaluate our approach on 38 misspecification scenarios spanning nine classes of optimization problems, several drawn from real-world applications, and show that TACIT can repair 78.9% of them (vs. 60.5% for the best baseline).

</details>


### [229] [A Parameter-Free Zeroth-Order Method with Covariance Matrix Adaptation and Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38561)
*Alexander Sholokhov,Alexander Rogozin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 POEM-CMA，一种参数自由的随机零阶优化算法，通过协方差矩阵对齐与经验有效维度实现各向异性采样，并获得近最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化用于梯度不可得或计算昂贵的黑箱问题，但传统方法依赖各向同性随机方向，采样效率低；本文旨在利用问题的低秩/内在低维结构提升采样效率。

Method: 在 POEM 基础上引入协方差矩阵对齐，用梯度估计构造协方差矩阵进行各向异性采样；定义经验有效维度 d* = tr(Σ̂)/λmax(Σ̂)，在采样与复杂度分析中用 d* 替代环境维度 d，且保持全参数自由。

Result: 证明 POEM-CMA 达到近最优收敛速率，仅需 Õ(d* κ(Σ̂) L² D_X² / ε²) 次随机零阶 oracle 查询；在低秩结构（d* ≪ d）问题上显著优于原始 POEM；基于 LibSVM 数据集的 hinge loss 二分类实验验证了其实际优越性。

Conclusion: POEM-CMA 通过协方差对齐与有效维度刻画，在保持参数自由的同时实现了对低内在维度问题的高效零阶优化，理论与实验均表明优于原有方法。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization methods are essential for solving black-box problems where gradient information is unavailable or expensive to compute. This paper presents POEM-CMA, a novel parameter-free stochastic zeroth-order algorithm that extends the recent POEM method by integrating covariance matrix alignment and the notion of effective dimension.
  In contrast to traditional zeroth-order approaches that rely on isotropic random directions, POEM-CMA performs anisotropic sampling by constructing a covariance matrix from gradient estimates. This enables the algorithm to focus sampling efforts on the most informative directions. We introduce the use of the empirical effective dimension $d^* = \frac{\operatorname{tr}(\hatΣ)}{λ_{\max}(\hatΣ)}$, which reflects the intrinsic dimensionality of the problem and replaces the ambient dimension in both sampling and complexity analysis.
  We prove that POEM-CMA achieves a near-optimal convergence rate, requiring only $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{d^* κ(\hatΣ) L^2 D_{\mathcal{X}}^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ stochastic zeroth-order oracle queries. The method remains fully parameter-free and demonstrates significant improvements over the original POEM in problems with low-rank structure where $d^* \ll d$. Numerical experiments on hinge-loss binary classification tasks using LibSVM datasets confirm the practical superiority of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [230] [Kyle meets time-inconsistency: a dynamic mean--variance informed trading problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38701)
*Shuoqing Deng,Zhichao Luo,Zhenhua Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究知情交易者具有动态均值—方差偏好（导致时间不一致）的Kyle-Back模型，将其建模为个体内部博弈，同时满足交易—定价均衡与时间一致均衡，刻画了均衡策略与价格冲击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知情交易者的动态均值—方差偏好会引发时间不一致问题，传统（时间一致）Kyle-Back框架无法直接适用于此情形，需要引入个体内部博弈刻画相继自我之间的均衡。

Method: 在Cho (2003)框架下，同时施加知情交易者与做市商之间的交易—定价均衡条件以及交易者相继自我之间的时间一致均衡条件；分别处理风险中性与风险厌恶两类知情交易者。核心技巧是将均衡条件约化为前向—后向常微分方程组，并用打靶法（shooting argument）处理边界条件。

Result: 风险中性情形下，显式给出均衡交易策略与价格冲击；风险厌恶情形下，证明均衡存在性，并通过非线性常微分方程耦合系统刻画均衡。

Conclusion: 均值—方差偏好重塑了"利用当前私有信息"与"保持未来信息优势"之间的跨期权衡，产生随时间递减的价格冲击期限结构，并使信息揭示和知情交易利润前移至交易期早期。

Abstract: We study a Kyle--Back model in which the informed trader has dynamic mean--variance preferences, leading to a time-inconsistent trading problem that is formulated as an intrapersonal game. The model combines two equilibrium requirements: a trading--pricing equilibrium between the informed trader and the market maker, and a time-consistent equilibrium among the trader's successive selves. Following the framework of Cho (2003), we consider both risk-neutral and risk-averse informed traders. In the risk-neutral case, we derive the equilibrium trading strategy and price impact explicitly. In the risk-averse case, we establish existence and characterize the equilibrium through a coupled system of nonlinear ordinary differential equations. Our main technique is to reduce the equilibrium conditions to a forward--backward ODE system and resolve the resulting boundary conditions by a shooting argument. Economically, the mean--variance preference reshapes the intertemporal trade-off between exploiting current private information and preserving future informational advantage, generating a declining term structure of price impact and shifting both information revelation and informed-trading profits toward earlier stages of the trading horizon.

</details>


### [231] [Decentralized Decision-Making among Heterogeneous Autonomous Vehicles: An $α$-Potential Game Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38731)
*Anran Hu,Zhexin Wang,Yufei Zhang,Xuan Di*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 $\alpha$-potential game 框架，将异构自主车辆间近似纳什均衡计算转化为单一 $\alpha$-potential 函数的最小化，并给出均衡近似误差与交互不对称性的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构自主车辆的多车博弈中，各车采用基于自身状态的去中心化闭环策略，且目标函数通过可能不对称的交互权重相互耦合；这类非对称交互使得均衡求解困难，且不对称性对均衡近似质量与系统效率的影响尚不清晰。

Method: 构建 $\alpha$-potential game 框架：显式构造 $\alpha$-potential 函数，证明其极小值点的存在性；将均衡近似误差 $\alpha$ 用交互不对称性刻画；引入车辆特定的缩放（vehicle-specific scaling）以降低有效交互不对称性，从而收紧近似误差并在若干重要情形下恢复精确 Nash 均衡；进一步推导 potential 所选策略的社会效率保证。

Result: 建立了近似 Nash 均衡与 $\alpha$-potential 最小化之间的等价归约，给出了 $\alpha$ 与交互不对称性的显式关系；vehicle-specific scaling 能收紧均衡近似并在重要情形下恢复精确 NE；得到了刻画最坏情况效率的社会效率保证；数值实验验证了框架在异构车辆交互、避碰避障、换道超车（不同交通配置）及基于优先级的交叉口通行中的灵活性。

Conclusion: $\alpha$-potential 框架为异构自主车辆的非对称交互多车博弈提供了可计算的近似均衡求解途径，并揭示交互结构对社会效率的影响；通过缩放设计可改善均衡近似质量，框架具备广泛的交通场景适用性。

Abstract: We study noncooperative multi-vehicle games among heterogeneous autonomous vehicles, where each vehicle adopts a decentralized closed-loop policy based on its own state, and optimizes an objective that depends on other vehicles through potentially asymmetric interaction weights. We develop an $α$-potential game framework that reduces the computation of an approximate Nash equilibrium (NE) to the minimization of a single auxiliary $α$-potential function. We explicitly construct this $α$-potential, establish the existence of its minimizers, and characterize the equilibrium approximation error $α$ in terms of interaction asymmetry. We further introduce vehicle-specific scaling to reduce the effective interaction asymmetry, thereby tightening the equilibrium approximation and, in important cases, recovering an exact NE despite asymmetric interactions. We also derive social-efficiency guarantees for the potential-selected policies, revealing how the interaction structure shapes worst-case efficiency. Numerical experiments demonstrate the flexibility of the framework in capturing heterogeneous vehicle interactions, collision and obstacle avoidance, lane changing and overtaking under different traffic configurations, and priority-based intersection crossing.

</details>


### [232] [ABrA-GD: Adaptive Bregman Accelerated Gradient Descent for Relatively Smooth and (Strongly-)Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38741)
*Damien Scieur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出自适应算法 ABrA-GD，用于相对光滑凸优化，基于可计算原始-对偶证书实现平滑性与几何自适应，达到加速 O(1/k²) 与线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对相对光滑凸优化问题，现有方法难以同时自适应平滑性与几何结构，且缺乏仅依赖少量问题相关参数的自适应机制，因此需要一种可计算的证书来指导加速算法的设计。

Method: 提出 ABrA-GD（自适应 Bregman 加速梯度下降）。核心是构造可计算的原始-对偶证书，通过比较目标值与正则化目标最小值的下界来保证进展；仅以相对强凸性常数为问题相关输入，实现平滑性与几何的自适应。并引入对偶 Bregman 长度畸变因子（BLDF），衡量对偶 Bregman 长度在锚点平移或重缩放下的变化。

Result: 在有界的局部对偶 BLDF 假设下，ABrA-GD 对凸目标取得加速的 O(1/k²) 收敛率，对相对强凸目标取得加速的线性收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作通过原始-对偶证书与对偶 BLDF 分析，统一实现了相对光滑优化中的平滑性与几何自适应，并获得了加速收敛保证。

Abstract: We propose ABrA-GD (Adaptive Bregman Accelerated Gradient Descent), an adaptive algorithm for relatively smooth convex optimization. The algorithm is derived from a computable primal--dual certificate that guarantees progress by comparing the objective value with a lower bound on the minimum of a regularized objective. This certificate enables adaptation to both smoothness and geometry, using only the relative strong convexity constant as a problem-dependent input. We introduce the dual Bregman Length Distortion Factor (BLDF), which measures how dual Bregman lengths change under an anchor shift or rescaling. Under bounded local dual BLDF, ABrA-GD achieves an accelerated $O(1/k^2)$ rate for convex objectives and an accelerated linear rate for relatively strongly convex objectives.

</details>


### [233] [Model-free Reinforcement Learning for Continuous Time and State: A Stochastic Maximum Principle Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38745)
*Lijun Bo,Yijie Huang,Jingfei Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间、连续状态与动作空间的随机控制问题，基于随机最大值原理开发了一种无模型强化学习算法，通过学习哈密顿量梯度并将其嵌入不精确梯度的策略梯度框架，建立了收敛性与误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间随机控制问题中，状态与动作空间连续，传统方法难以直接求解；随机最大值原理给出了最优策略的一阶条件，但涉及伴随倒向随机微分方程，需要解耦场的存在性以保证哈密顿量梯度可表示为时间与状态的确定性函数，从而支持基于数据的无模型学习。

Method: 1）针对参数化马尔可夫策略，证明伴随 BSDE 解耦场的存在性，使哈密顿量梯度可表示为时间与状态的确定性函数；2）直接从数据中学习该哈密顿量梯度；3）将其嵌入不精确梯度的策略梯度方案中。

Result: 证明了所提策略梯度算法的收敛性并给出误差估计；在学习率、探索参数与逼近误差满足适当条件时，目标值收敛且策略梯度的 L2 范数渐近趋于零；进一步证明所提基于 SMP 的策略梯度表示与基于优势率函数的连续时间确定性策略梯度表示等价；数值实验验证了算法的有效性与效率。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连续时间随机控制中基于随机最大值原理的无模型策略梯度方法的理论基础与收敛保证，并揭示了其与确定性策略梯度表示的内在联系，为连续时空强化学习提供了可行且高效的新途径。

Abstract: This paper develops a model-free reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm based on the stochastic maximum principle for continuous-time stochastic control problems with continuous state and action spaces. For a parameterized Markovian policy, we establish the existence of the decoupling field for the adjoint backward stochastic differential equation, which allows the Hamiltonian gradient to be represented as a deterministic function of time and state. We then learn this Hamiltonian gradient directly from data and incorporate it into a policy gradient scheme with inexact gradients. We establish the convergence of the resulting policy gradient algorithm and establish the corresponding error estimates. Under suitable conditions on learning rates, exploration parameters, and approximation errors, the objective values converge and the $L^2$-norm of the policy gradient vanishes asymptotically. We further show that the proposed SMP-based policy gradient representation is equivalent to the existing continuous-time deterministic policy gradient representation based on the advantage-rate function. The effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed RL algorithm are demonstrated by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [234] [Reclassified to Conform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38754)
*Chris Martin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将跨多年多份财报的财务报表明细行重建问题建模为整数规划优化问题：先以"标签数字连续性"构建strand，再在相邻申报边界列举候选编辑并定价，用整数规划选出最便宜的编辑集合，从而把每条明细行贯穿各年追踪出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公司财务报表需从多年多份申报文件中拼装，但每份申报可能重命名、合并、拆分或移动明细行，且仅重述最近两三年以匹配；申报之间缺乏一致、机器可读的映射，行的标签乃至XBRL标签都不固定。因此需要一个自动化方法重建可跨年比较的报表。

Method: 将重建视为优化问题：(1) 若某标签从一份申报到下一份保持数字不变，则视为同一行，这类连续段先连接成"strand"，之后才权衡编辑；(2) 在每两个申报的边界处，列出通过其类型所需校验的候选编辑，每个编辑附带其能解释的strand端点以及"要求读者相信超出申报所显示内容"的价格；(3) 用整数规划选出最便宜且能将每条行恰好解释一次的编辑集合，所选编辑即可追踪每条行穿越各申报的路径。

Result: 在随机抽取的200家公司上，可校验的总计中有99.9%能够加总正确；在有可用供应商对照的124家中，本方法追踪的明细行与供应商序列在双方均填充的单元格上一致率达96%，比仅用标签多覆盖10%的单元格，且在这些多出的单元格上与供应商一致率达90%；方法还能给出每家公司报告变更的记录。

Conclusion: 该方法生成的报表使各行跨所有年份遵循同一条明细行，在每份申报的重述窗口内可比，并在数字被重述或推导处加以标注；可适配任一份申报的版式，每个数字都引用陈述它的申报，每个声明的总计都重新校验，同时输出公司报告变更记录。

Abstract: A company's financial statements over many years must be assembled from many filings. Each filing may rename, combine, split or move its lines, and restates only the last two or three years to match. Filings provide no consistent, machine-readable mapping between successive statements, and neither a line's label nor its XBRL tag is fixed. We treat the reconstruction as an optimization problem. A tag that keeps its numbers from one filing to the next is taken to be the same line, and such runs are joined into strands before any edit is weighed. At each boundary between filings, the candidate edits that pass the check their kind requires are listed, each with the strand endpoints it would explain and a price for how much it asks the reader to believe beyond what the filings show. An integer program picks the cheapest set of the listed edits that accounts for every line exactly once, and the chosen edits trace each line through the filings. The result is a statement whose rows follow one line across every year, comparable within each filing's restated window and marked where a number was restated or derived, in any filing's layout, with each number cited to the filing that stated it and each declared total re-checked. On 200 companies drawn at random, 99.9% of the totals that can be checked add up. Among the 124 with a usable vendor comparison, the lines the method follows agree with the vendor's series on 96% of the cells both fill, reach 10% more cells than a tag alone and agree with the vendor on 90% of those. The method also yields a record of what each company changed in its reporting.

</details>


### [235] [Robust Planning with Quadratically Constrained State-Dependent Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38803)
*Fabio Spada,Behçet Açıkmeşe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带状态约束、且控制/状态相关不确定输入与测量的鲁棒轨迹规划问题。通过拉格朗日对偶推导出保证状态约束鲁棒满足的有限非光滑约束，避免使用双层求解器；进而提出两步凸启发式方法（凸松弛＋凸可行性恢复）求解该非凸非光滑问题。在状态相关导航不确定的交会场景中验证，相比Gurobi商用求解器计算时间减少13.8倍，同时保持约束的鲁棒满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒轨迹规划需在不确定性下保证状态约束满足，但不确定输入/测量与状态相关、且约束形式导致问题成为非凸非光滑甚至需要双层求解，计算困难，因此需要更高效且能保证鲁棒性的建模与求解方法。

Method: 不确定输入与测量满足带秩-2不定乘子的二次不等式，测量依赖于不确定状态实现及不确定输入；利用拉格朗日对偶将鲁棒约束转化为有限个非光滑约束以获得单层可行表述；针对所得非凸非光滑问题，设计两步凸启发式：先做凸松弛，再进行凸可行性恢复。

Result: 在带状态相关导航不确定性的交会场景中，两步方法相比商用求解器Gurobi将计算时间降低13.8倍，同时仍保持约束的鲁棒满足。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日对偶可将状态相关不确定性下的鲁棒轨迹规划化为无需双层求解器的有限非光滑约束形式，配合两步凸启发式可在保持鲁棒约束满足的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: We address robust trajectory-planning problems with state constraints and with control/state-dependent uncertain inputs and measurements. Uncertain inputs and measurements satisfy quadratic inequalities with rank-2 indefinite multipliers; measurements depend on the uncertain-state realization and on the uncertain inputs. By means of Lagrangian duality, we derive finite nonsmooth constraints that guarantee robust satisfaction of the state constraints, yielding a formulation that can be addressed without bilevel solvers. We develop a two-step convex heuristic for the resulting nonconvex nonsmooth formulation, using a convex relaxation followed by a convex feasibility-recovery step. We test the two-step approach on a rendezvous scenario with state-dependent navigation uncertainty, and we reduce by a factor 13.8 the computation time with respect to the commercial solver Gurobi, while maintaining robust satisfaction of constraints.

</details>


### [236] [Halpern-Accelerated Majorized ADMM with Optimal Non-Ergodic Convergence under Degenerate Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38808)
*Ning Zhang,Benqi Liu,Liwei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带可能非正定近端项的Halpern加速majorized ADMM，用于线性约束凸复合优化，并给出O(1/k)的残差收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: majorization可简化ADMM子问题，但会引入前向扰动；同时非正定近端项能提升灵活性却可能破坏度量性质，因此需要在理论上刻画其收敛性与不动点结构。

Method: 将majorized ADMM重构为带扰动的退化近端点方法，刻划诱导映射的不动点为KKT解；在range compatibility与metric cocoercivity条件下证明映射在可能奇异预条件子诱导的半范数下非扩张；有限维中给出可验证的块矩阵条件以允许非正定近端正则化；采用松弛Halpern迭代。

Result: 松弛Halpern迭代得到O(1/k)的不动点残差界，在一般非扩张设定下是sharp的；并在中间迭代处得到O(1/k)的非遍历KKT残差界。数值实验验证了预测的残差衰减及majorization与非正定近端项的优势。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并扩展了majorized ADMM的收敛分析，在保留非正定近端灵活性的同时给出了最优阶的残差收敛率。

Abstract: We develop a Halpern-accelerated majorized alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) with possibly indefinite proximal terms for linearly constrained convex composite optimization. Majorization simplifies the subproblems but introduces a forward perturbation to the standard degenerate proximal point representation. We reformulate majorized ADMM as a perturbed degenerate proximal point method and characterize the fixed points of the induced mapping as Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) solutions. Under range compatibility and metric cocoercivity conditions, the mapping is nonexpansive in the seminorm induced by a possibly singular preconditioner. In finite dimensions, a verifiable block-matrix condition permits indefinite proximal regularization while retaining the required metric properties. A relaxed Halpern iteration then yields an $\mathcal O(1/k)$ fixed-point residual bound, sharp in the general nonexpansive setting, and an $\mathcal O(1/k)$ nonergodic KKT residual bound at the intermediate iterates. Numerical experiments illustrate the predicted residual decay and the benefits of majorization and indefinite proximal terms.

</details>


### [237] [Boundary Geometry and Global Rates of Cyclic Coordinate Descent for SVM Duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39240)
*Egor Gladin,Eva Ibodova*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确分析盒约束凸二次规划的循环坐标最小化（CCM），提出融合坐标次序与边界几何的全局收缩界，严格改进了Wang-Lin误差界因子，并揭示仅依赖Hessian的界在奇异情形下的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环坐标下降广泛用于训练SVM，但现有全局线性收敛结论仅给出渐近速率，无法刻画具体实例上的收缩行为；且多数分析依赖Hessian信息，忽略坐标更新次序与边界几何，对奇异Hessian、非唯一解与边界截断更新缺乏有效界。

Method: 针对盒约束凸二次问题研究精确循环坐标最小化，构造全局收缩界：以归一化Hessian的三角部分刻画相邻坐标更新间的交互，以最优性间隙上的矩阵下界刻画目标增长；分析在奇异Hessian、非唯一极小点及边界截断更新下依然有效，并给出误差界特例化与逐实例界的实用计算流程。

Result: 在同一误差界常数下，误差界特例化严格锐化了Wang-Lin因子；正定情形下证明一次Gauss-Seidel扫描的最坏收缩率仍是盒约束下的全局上界，且在内点最优时取等号；构造两坐标族，其归一化Hessian固定且奇异，随边界余量趋于零，最坏收缩率趋于1，说明仅靠Hessian的界本质不足；并在多个经典SVM数据集上完成数值验证。

Conclusion: 循环坐标最小化的收敛速率由Hessian交互结构与边界几何共同决定，仅凭Hessian无法给出紧界；所提框架可对单个实例给出更紧的收缩率估计，为SVM等盒约束二次问题的坐标类算法提供更精确的收敛理论。

Abstract: Cyclic coordinate descent is widely used to train support vector machines, but global linear convergence alone gives limited guidance about the rate on a particular instance. We study exact cyclic coordinate minimization for box-constrained convex quadratics and develop global contraction bounds that capture both coordinate order and boundary geometry. Our convergence certificate combines two ingredients: interactions between successive coordinate updates, encoded by the triangular part of the normalized Hessian, and objective growth, captured by a matrix lower bound on the optimality gap. It remains valid for cases with singular Hessians, nonunique minimizers, and updates clipped at the boundary. An error-bound specialization of our analysis strictly sharpens the Wang-Lin factor when both use the same error-bound constant. In the positive-definite case, the analysis shows that the worst-case contraction of one Gauss-Seidel sweep remains a global upper bound under box constraints, with equality for an interior optimizer. We also demonstrate the limitations of Hessian-only bounds by constructing a two-coordinate family with a fixed singular normalized Hessian, whose worst-case contraction approaches one as its boundary margin vanishes. The theory is complemented by practical procedures for bounding the worst-case contraction on individual instances and numerical evaluation on several classical SVM datasets.

</details>


### [238] [Optimal Refinement in Dynamic Discretisation Discovery for Continuous-Time Service Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39269)
*Alexander Helber,Oliver Bachtler,Tom Wüllner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究动态离散化发现（DDD）框架中的最优精细化问题，针对连续时间服务网络设计问题（CTSNDP），提出冲突的紧凑伪多项式表示及精确求解方法，使模型规模和运行时间降低约10–20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD通过迭代求解并精细化松弛问题来求解原问题，是CTSNDP等问题的先进算法基础。精细化步骤需识别松弛解中导致原问题不可行的冲突，并施加修正以避免其再次出现。如何从候选修正集合中选出能覆盖全部冲突且使松弛规模最小的子集，直接影响松弛问题求解难度，是亟待优化的关键问题。

Method: 作者将精细化问题形式化为最优精细化问题：从一组修正中选择子集，使其修复所有冲突并得到规模最小的松弛。尽管冲突数量在实例参数上可能呈指数级，作者提出用伪多项式空间和时间对其进行紧凑表示，并基于该紧凑表示设计精确求解方法，与自然的整数规划建模进行对比。

Result: 最优方法能在可忽略的时间内找到小规模松弛，在某些实例类上比自然整数规划公式快100倍以上。将该精细化方法用于CTSNDP的DDD算法后，与现有方法相比，模型规模和运行时间均降低约10%–20%。

Conclusion: 基于冲突紧凑表示的精确精细化方法显著加速了松弛选择过程，并在CTSNDP的DDD算法中带来模型规模与求解时间的双重缩减，验证了该紧凑表示与精确方法的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Dynamic Discretisation Discovery (DDD) is a framework for solving a problem by iteratively solving and refining a relaxed version of the problem. DDD is the basis for state-of-the-art algorithms for various problems, including the continuous-time service network design problem (CTSNDP), which we study here. In the refinement step, such algorithms must identify conflicts in the relaxation solution that make it infeasible for the original problem and then apply fixes to the relaxation to prevent them from recurring. We study the optimal refinement problem of selecting from a set of fixes a subset that fixes all conflicts and yields a relaxation of minimum size, with the aim of making it easier to solve. We demonstrate that while the number of conflicts may be exponential in the instance parameters, we can represent them more compactly, using only pseudo-polynomial space and time. Based on our more compact representation, we develop exact methods for solving the refinement problem. The best method allows us to find small relaxations in negligible amounts of time, more than 100 times faster than a natural integer programming formulation for some instance classes. Using this refinement approach in a DDD algorithm for the CTSNDP, we observe reductions in model sizes and running times of about 10 to 20 % compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [239] [Generalized Geometry Block Proximal Linearized Method for Multiblock Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39301)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出广义几何块近端线性化方法（GGBPL）及其惯性版本（iGGBPL），突破传统欧氏几何限制，用于求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题，并给出收敛性与复杂度保证及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多块非凸非光滑优化方法在标准欧氏几何中构造近端线性化算子，使块变量更新依赖标准内积及其诱导范数，无法刻画目标问题的几何结构，导致数值效率低。

Method: 提出广义几何近端线性化算子更新块变量，并据此构建 GGBPL 方法；算子允许使用任意内积与一般可容许度量构造块代理函数，从而适配不同问题的几何结构。进一步引入惯性版本 iGGBPL。建立广义几何下的统一收敛框架。

Result: 在该框架下证明目标函数值收敛、生成序列全局收敛到临界点，并给出收敛速率；获得达到 ε-稳定点的 O(ε^{-2}) 迭代复杂度界。将方法应用于带 ℓ0 约束的稀疏非负矩阵分解和带 ℓ0 约束的稀疏非负 CP 分解，数值结果优于多种先进方法。

Conclusion: 广义几何近端线性化框架能有效利用问题几何结构，提升多块非凸非光滑优化的求解效率，并具备完整的收敛性与复杂度理论保障。

Abstract: This paper considers a class of multiblock nonconvex and nonsmooth optimization problems arising in many applications. Existing methods construct proximal linearized operators or their variants within standard Euclidean geometry to solve this class of problems, forcing their block variable updates to rely on the standard inner product and its induced norm. Nevertheless, this construction fails to capture the geometric structure of the target problem, leading to low numerical efficiency. To overcome these drawbacks, we propose a generalized geometry proximal linearized operator for updating block variables, and develop the Generalized Geometry Block Proximal Linearized (GGBPL) method based on this operator. Compared with existing proximal linearized operators, the proposed operator allows the block surrogate functions to be constructed using arbitrary inner products and general admissible metrics, thereby enabling the GGBPL method to adapt its updates to the geometric structure of various problems. We also introduce the inertial version of GGBPL, named the inertial GGBPL (iGGBPL) method. We further establish a new unified convergence framework under this generalized geometry, within which we prove that our methods guarantee convergence of the objective function values, establish global convergence of the generated sequence to a critical point, and derive the convergence rate of our methods. We also establish an $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration complexity bound for obtaining an $\varepsilon$-stationary point. We apply our methods to two nonconvex and nonsmooth problems: sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. Numerical results demonstrate the superior numerical performance of our proposed methods over several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [240] [Robust forms for passive descriptor systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39465)
*Peter Benner,Anshul Prajapati,Paul Van Dooren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出计算广义状态空间模型的被动半径方法，并以此构造鲁棒的descriptor实现：连续时间下为端口-哈密顿descriptor形式，离散时间下为归一化实现，其计算与表征被动的线性矩阵不等式特解相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动的有理传递函数的广义状态空间模型在实际中常受参数扰动影响，需要量化系统保持被动的裕度（被动半径），并据此设计在扰动下仍保持被动性的鲁棒实现。

Method: 针对连续时间与离散时间两种情形，计算给定广义状态空间模型（descriptor形式）的被动半径；利用被动半径构造鲁棒descriptor实现，其关键在于求解刻画传递函数被动性的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的一个特解。

Result: 给出了连续时间与离散时间下被动半径的计算方法，并实现了相应的鲁棒descriptor实现：连续时间情形得到端口-哈密顿descriptor形式，离散时间情形得到归一化实现。

Conclusion: 被动半径的计算与鲁棒实现的构造在连续和离散两种情形下均归结为被动性LMI的一个特解，从而统一了鲁棒实现的设计框架。

Abstract: We show how to compute the passivity radius of a given generalized state-space model of a rational transfer function that represents a passive system, both in the continuous-time and discrete-time cases. We then use this to construct robust descriptor realizations. For the continuous-time case, these realizations have a port-Hamiltonian descriptor form. For the discrete-time case a similar result holds for the so-called normalized realizations. The computation of such robust realizations is linked in both these cases to a particular solution of a linear matrix inequality that characterizes the passivity of the transfer function.

</details>


### [241] [Aligning feature-mapping designs to given density fields, first and second order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39472)
*Fabian Wein,Patrick Jung,Arash Moradian,Michael Stingl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于梯度的分阶段最小二乘跟踪方法，将特征映射构型对齐到给定（伪）密度场以改善初始化；引入基于自动参数化Bézier曲线的非对称过渡函数处理孤立特征灵敏度不足问题，并首次给出特征映射的Hessian公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征映射方法虽比SIMP更具可解释性与参数化优势，但其优化对初始设计敏感；因此需要一种将特征映射构型对齐到给定（伪）密度场的方法，例如用于后续特征映射优化的初始化。

Method: 采用基于梯度的分阶段最小二乘跟踪（least-squares tracking）公式，前一阶段仅奖励对齐而不惩罚失配；由于无有限元仿真迭代成本低但孤立特征灵敏度信息少，提出基于自动参数化Bézier曲线的非对称过渡函数。并首次给出特征映射的Hessian公式，以及基于状态柔度的精确Hessian，其每个特征变量需额外一次FEM系统求解；同时展示基于几何投影法特征缩放变量的可选特征最小化。

Result: 在2D悬臂与五杆设计上验证方法有效；方法适用于一阶优化器，同时因无状态问题且变量少，二阶公式同样具有吸引力；报告了一阶与二阶优化器在基准问题上的表现。

Conclusion: 该分阶段梯度方法可有效将特征映射配置对齐到给定密度场，非对称过渡函数缓解孤立特征灵敏度不足，首次提出的Hessian公式为特征映射优化提供了二阶途径。

Abstract: Feature-mapping methods represent structural designs by explicit geometric primitives on fixed analysis grids, an interpretable and parametric alternative to the density fields of density-based topology optimization (SIMP). In contrast to SIMP, however, feature-mapping optimization is sensitive to the initial design.
  We present a gradient-based approach to align a feature-mapping configuration to a given (pseudo) density field, e.g., to initialize a subsequent feature-mapping optimization. The staged approach is based on a least-squares tracking formulation, preceded by a variant that only rewards alignment and does not penalize mismatch. Without an underlying finite element simulation, iterations are cheap, but isolated features receive little sensitivity information. As a remedy, we propose an asymmetric transition function based on automatically parametrized Bézier curves. We demonstrate the approach for a 2D cantilever and the five-bar design; for the latter, we show optional feature minimization based on the feature scaling variable of the geometry projection method.
  The approach works well with first-order optimizers. The absence of a state problem and the small number of variables, however, also make a second-order formulation attractive. To the best of the authors' knowledge, we present the first Hessian formulation for feature mapping and report the behavior of first- and second-order optimizers on our benchmark problems. For completeness, we also give the exact Hessian of the state-based compliance, which requires one additional solution of the FEM system per feature variable.

</details>


### [242] [The Maximal Robust Positively Invariant Set for Linear Difference Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39499)
*Kai Wang,Daniel Axehill*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为受硬状态约束的线性差分包含系统，构建了最大鲁棒正不变集的系统刻画与精确计算框架，证明了其可表示为鲁棒前驱算子的最大不动点，并给出三种等价递减集迭代及精确停止判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受硬状态约束的线性差分包含系统的最大鲁棒正不变集，其精确刻画与有限步计算条件尚需系统化：既要给出集合论层面的等价迭代与停止准则，也要刻画有限确定性与非空性条件，并针对多面体约束设计可精确实现的算法。

Method: 将最大鲁棒正不变集刻画为鲁棒前驱算子的最大不动点，导出三种等价递减集迭代——标准迭代、自限制迭代与增量迭代，并给出精确停止测试；在矩阵族一致指数稳定与扰动有界下刻画极限扰动可达集，据此推导非空性与有限确定条件；对多面体状态约束，在有界扰动及有限或凸多面体矩阵族情形下给出三种迭代的精确半空间实现，并进行复杂度分析与数值研究。

Result: 给出了最大鲁棒正不变集的等价迭代刻画与精确停止判据；在一致指数稳定与有界扰动下获得非空性与有限确定性的充分条件；对多面体约束给出精确半空间实现；复杂度分析与数值实验表明三种实现生成相同集合序列，但在计算量分布上存在差异，且问题结构可指导实现方式的选择。

Conclusion: 该框架统一刻画了最大鲁棒正不变集的集合论性质与可计算实现，说明不同等价迭代在保证结果一致的同时具有不同的计算代价分配，实际中应依据底层问题结构选择合适实现。

Abstract: This article develops a systematic characterization and exact computational framework for the maximal robust positively invariant set of linear difference inclusions subject to hard state constraints. The set is characterized as the greatest fixed point of the robust predecessor operator, leading to three equivalent decreasing set iterations---the standard, self-restricted, and incremental iterations---and exact stopping tests. Under uniform exponential stability of the matrix family and bounded disturbances, the limiting disturbance-reachable set is characterized and used to derive conditions for nonemptiness and finite determination. When the state constraint set is polyhedral, the three iterations admit exact half-space implementations for bounded disturbances and finite or polytopic matrix families. Although these implementations generate the same set sequence, they distribute computational effort differently. The complexity analysis and numerical study make these differences explicit and reveal how the underlying problem structure informs the choice of implementation.

</details>


### [243] [Several Accelerated and Stable Pseudo-Energy-Dissipative Lagrange Multiplier Methods based on Second-order Flow and Variable Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39695)
*Xiaoli Li,Jianghua Liu,Zhiping Mao,Jingrong Wei,Xuan Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于二阶惯性动力学，提出两种加速拉格朗日乘子（LM）优化方法，用于无约束非凸优化，并给出收敛性与大量数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸优化方法在逃离局部极小、收敛性保证及对初始学习率敏感等方面仍存在不足，尤其在科学机器学习（PINNs、DeepONets）训练中对优化器的鲁棒性与精度要求较高。作者希望借助二阶惯性动力学与拉格朗日乘子框架，构建兼具加速性、理论收敛保证和数值稳健性的优化方法。

Method: 1) 基于二阶惯性动力学，提出两种加速的LM优化方法：Linear LM-based second-order flow method 和 linear LM-based variable and operator splitting method；2) 针对两种方法分别提出松弛（relaxed）与自适应（adaptive）变体，以防止拉格朗日乘子退化并实现时间步长的自动调整；3) 在新提出的二阶LM优化框架下，构造伪能量泛函（pseudo-energy functional），证明其沿迭代轨迹单调耗散，并严格建立迭代序列收敛到稳定点的结论。

Result: 在函数优化和偏微分方程基准问题上的大量数值实验表明，所提方法能够有效逃离局部极小、达到高精度；在作为PINNs和DeepONets优化器时，对神经网络训练初始学习率的选择表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶LM优化框架在理论（伪能量单调耗散与收敛到稳定点）与实验（逃离局部极小、高精度、对初始学习率鲁棒）两方面均得到验证，适用于非凸优化及科学机器学习中的神经网络训练。

Abstract: Based on second-order inertial dynamics, we develop two accelerated Lagrange multiplier (LM)-based optimization methods: Linear LM-based second-order flow method and linear LM-based variable and operator splitting method for unconstrained non-convex optimization problems. Relaxed and adaptive variants of both methods are proposed to prevent degeneration of the Lagrange multiplier and to enable automatic adjustment of the time step size. Within the novel second-order LM-based optimization framework, the associated pseudo-energy functional is shown to dissipate monotonically along the iterative trajectory, and convergence of the generated iterates to stationary points is rigorously established. Extensive numerical experiments on function optimization and partial differential equation benchmark problems, including applications as optimizers for physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and deep operator networks (DeepONets), demonstrate that the proposed methods effectively escape local minima, achieve high accuracy, and exhibit strong robustness with respect to the choice of the initial learning rate during neural network training.

</details>


### [244] [Improved KKT Complexity for First-Order Bilevel Optimization under Weak Lower-Level Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39736)
*Jan Harold Alcantara,Masahiro Inoue,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出非精确变量平滑罚方法（IVSP）求解弱下层凸性下确定性一阶双层优化，基于δ-松弛Moreau间隙约束，在ENNAMCQ下证明罚参数有限稳定与$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$一阶复杂度，给出可验证充分条件，数值实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化方法多依赖强凸性、Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件或误差界性质，且精确Moreau间隙约束存在内在的约束限定退化问题，限制了对非凸下层目标的适用性。作者希望放宽这些假设，覆盖更一般的弱下层凸性场景。

Method: 构建δ-松弛Moreau间隙约束（δ>0）刻画下层稳定性条件，提出非精确变量平滑罚方法（IVSP）计算近似KKT点。在标准ENNAMCQ约束限定下分析自适应罚参数的有限稳定性及ε-KKT点的一阶复杂度，并针对凸下层目标（无非恒定仿射段，含严格凸情形）与非凸样本重加权模型给出ENNAMCQ可验证充分条件。

Result: 对任意固定松弛水平δ，在ENNAMCQ下证明自适应罚参数有限稳定，获得计算ε-KKT点的$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$一阶复杂度；正松弛避免精确Moreau间隙约束的约束限定退化，同时达到$\mathcal O(\sqrt\delta)$的下层近稳定性保证。合成与真实双层学习问题的数值实验验证IVSP实际性能。

Conclusion: IVSP在弱下层凸性下有效求解双层优化，兼顾理论复杂度保证与约束限定可行性，为含非凸下层目标的双层学习问题提供实用且具理论支撑的求解框架。

Abstract: We study deterministic first-order bilevel optimization under weak lower-level convexity, allowing nonconvex lower-level objectives and without assuming strong convexity, the Polyak-Łojasiewicz condition, or an error-bound property. We consider a $δ$-relaxed Moreau-gap constraint, with $δ>0$, for the lower-level stationarity condition and propose an inexact variable-smoothing penalty method (IVSP) for computing its approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points. For any fixed relaxation level $δ$, under a standard extended no-nonzero-abnormal-multiplier constraint qualification (ENNAMCQ), we prove finite stabilization of the adaptive penalty parameter and an overall $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ first-order complexity for computing an $\varepsilon$-KKT point. Notably, we give verifiable sufficient conditions for ENNAMCQ covering convex lower-level objectives without nonconstant affine segments (including the strictly convex case), and a class of nonconvex sample-reweighting models. The positive relaxation avoids the intrinsic constraint-qualification degeneracy of the exact Moreau-gap constraint while achieving an $\mathcal O(\sqrtδ)$ lower-level near-stationarity guarantee. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world bilevel learning problems illustrate the practical performance of IVSP.

</details>


### [245] [Sharp Quadratic Majorants of Weighted Power Means of Quadratic Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39921)
*Alexey Peregudin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究凹加权幂平均（作用于半正定二次型）何时具有无间隙的二次上界，并证明在下包络凸性条件下可由单一线性矩阵不等式精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量情形下，凹幂平均是其支撑仿射优函数的逐点下包络；但将标量变量替换为二次型后，这些界可能不再是所有二次优函数中的极值（失去尖锐性）。论文旨在刻画这种尖锐性能被保持的条件，并给出无间隙的凸证书。

Method: 以标量下包络表示（支撑仿射优函数）为出发点，将标量变量替换为半正定二次型；引入关于联合数值域的下包络凸性条件，在该条件下将非线性幂平均不等式等价转化为单个线性矩阵不等式（LMI）；并推导二次型幂平均和在单位球面上的变分公式，将极大化问题转为精确的凸特征值极小化问题。

Result: 证明在下包络凸性条件下尖锐性得以保持，非线性不等式可由单个LMI无间隙认证；该条件弱于全增广数值域的凸性，且对两个二次型自动成立。由此得到覆盖整个凹幂平均尺度的尖锐 Peter–Paul 型优函数，Yuan 引理与加权几何平均为其特例。变分公式在相同条件下使极大化等价于凸特征值极小化。有限时域控制算例展示了证书的构造性用法。

Conclusion: 论文给出刻画二次型凹幂平均尖锐二次上界的统一框架：下包络凸性是保证LMI无间隙证书的充分条件，既统一并推广了已有结果（Yuan引理、加权几何平均等），又通过变分公式与控制应用说明其可计算性与构造性价值。

Abstract: We study when concave weighted power means of positive semidefinite quadratic forms admit sharp quadratic upper bounds. The starting point is the scalar fact that each such mean is the lower envelope of its supporting affine majorants. After the scalar variables are replaced by quadratic forms, the resulting bounds may cease to be extremal among quadratic majorants. We prove that sharpness is preserved under a lower-envelope convexity condition on the joint numerical range. In that case, a nonlinear inequality involving power means of quadratic forms is certified, with no gap, by a single linear matrix inequality. The condition is weaker than convexity of the full augmented range and holds automatically for two quadratic forms. This yields a sharp Peter-Paul-type majorant for the whole concave power-mean scale, including Yuan's lemma and the weighted geometric mean as special cases. We also derive variational formulas for the extrema of sums of power means of quadratic forms on the unit sphere; under the same lower-envelope condition, the maximization becomes an exact convex eigenvalue minimization. A finite-horizon control application illustrates how the certificate can be used constructively.

</details>


### [246] [Input-to-state stability of second-order port-Hamiltonian systems under nonlinear dynamic boundary feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39948)
*Bouchra Elghazi,Birgit Jacob,Christophe Prieur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对一维空间域上无穷维二阶端口Hamilton系统的输入-状态稳定性问题，在非线性动态边界反馈与边界扰动下，利用端口Hamilton框架中的能量方法给出了闭环系统一致输入-状态稳定性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维端口Hamilton系统的稳定性分析多集中于线性或无扰动情形，边界处的非线性动态反馈与外部扰动共存时缺乏系统的稳定性判据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在端口Hamilton框架下采用能量方法（构造能量型Lyapunov泛函/耗散不等式），分析闭环系统在边界非线性动态反馈与边界扰动作用下的耗散性与稳定性。

Result: 得到一组充分条件，在该条件下闭环系统具有一致输入-状态稳定性（uniform ISS）。

Conclusion: 边界非线性动态反馈配合所给条件可保证受扰无穷维二阶端口Hamilton系统的一致输入-状态稳定性，能量方法在该框架下是有效的分析工具。

Abstract: The input-to-state stability of a class of infinite-dimensional second-order port-Hamiltonian systems on a one-dimensional spatial domain is analyzed under nonlinear dynamic boundary feedback and boundary disturbances. Using energy methods in the port-Hamiltonian framework, we obtain sufficient conditions that guarantee uniform input-to-state stability of the closed-loop system.

</details>


### [247] [ExactVariance of Random Return in Distributional LQR and Its Application to Mean-Variance Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39981)
*Ruyi Teng,Zifan Wang,Yulong Gao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将累积回报的方差引入离散时间分布LQR框架，首次推导出折扣无限时域回报方差的精确闭式表达，并提出均值-方差最优控制问题及伴随梯度下降算法求解，通过数值实验验证了风险-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LQR仅最小化期望累积回报，无法刻画性能波动，难以适用于风险敏感场景。因此需要将累积回报方差作为风险度量纳入LQR框架。

Method: 在离散时间分布LQR框架下，针对独立同分布且概率密度对称的扰动，推导折扣无限时域回报方差的精确闭式表达；对高斯扰动，该表达简化为仅依赖扰动协方差的形式。基于此构建均值-方差最优控制问题以显式权衡期望回报与性能波动，并针对所得非凸优化问题提出伴随梯度下降算法求解其惩罚形式。

Result: 获得首个折扣无限时域回报方差的精确闭式表达，高斯情形下简化为仅依赖扰动协方差；所提算法保证所有迭代点保持稳定并收敛至惩罚目标的驻点；数值实验验证了框架有效性及风险-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 将方差作为风险度量引入LQR可行且具有理论价值，所提闭式表达与伴随梯度下降算法为风险感知控制提供了有效工具，数值实验证实了风险与性能之间的内在权衡。

Abstract: The classical linear quadratic regulator (LQR) minimizes the expected cumulative return but fails to account for performance variability, rendering it inadequate for risk-aware applications. To address this, we introduce the variance of the cumulative return as a risk measure in LQR. We derive the first exact closed-form expression for the variance of the discounted in?finite horizon return within the discrete-time Distributional LQR framework, for i.i.d. disturbances with symmetric probability densities. For Gaussian disturbances, this expression elegantly simplifies to a form dependent only on the disturbance covariance. Leveraging these theoretical foundations, we formulate a mean-variance optimal control problem that explicitly manages the trade-off between expected return and performance variability. To address the resulting non-convex optimization problem, we propose a novel adjoint gradient descent algorithm for a penalized formulation of the original problem, and establish that all iterates remain stabilizing and converge to a stationary point of the penalized objective. The effectiveness of this framework and the inherent risk-performance trade-off? are demonstrated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [248] [EndoWake: Modeling Wind by Linear Programming for Wind Farm Layout Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40157)
*Martina Fischetti,Matteo Fischetti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出内生尾流模型EndoWake，将各格点风速视为决策变量并通过线性传播约束关联上风向邻点，从而把尾流方程直接嵌入MILP；开发了"带状"有效不等式强化根界，并设计branch-and-check算法（主问题保留选址变量与hypograph变量，精确线性时间oracle评估整数解，使用问题特定的upwind cuts），在方形网格与四扇区风向上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场布局优化的核心难点是尾流效应：它显著影响发电量，且其函数形式复杂，难以精确评估并嵌入优化框架。传统做法往往依赖非线性或近似处理，导致模型难以求解或精度不足，因此需要一种能够兼顾精确性与可解性的建模与求解方法。

Method: 提出内生尾流模型EndoWake，将每个格点的风速作为决策变量，通过与上风向邻点的线性传播约束建立联系，使尾流方程可直接纳入混合整数线性规划（MILP）。在此基础上构建MILP模型，并引入新的"带状"（band）有效不等式以显著强化根节点界。进一步设计branch-and-check框架：主问题仅保留选址变量及每单元、每情景一个hypograph变量；整数解由精确的线性时间oracle评估；最优性割为问题特定的"上风向割"（upwind cuts）。

Result: 在阶梯式方形网格、四扇区轴向风玫瑰情景下开展计算实验，结果表明所提出的EndoWake模型、band不等式及branch-and-check方案能够有效提升求解表现。

Conclusion: 将尾流影响内生化并线性化嵌入MILP是可行的，配合强化有效不等式与问题特定割的branch-and-check框架，可在保证尾流评估精确性的同时改善优化求解效率，为风电场布局优化提供了一种新的精确建模与求解途径。

Abstract: The Wind Farm Layout Optimization problem consists of placing a given number of turbines within a given area so as to maximize energy production. One of its main challenges is the wake effect between turbines, which can strongly affect production and is a cumbersome function to evaluate and to embed in an optimization framework. In this paper we introduce a new endogenous wake model (EndoWake), based on the idea of treating the wind speed at every grid point as a decision variable, linked to its upwind neighbors through linear propagation constraints. This makes it possible to incorporate the wake equations directly into a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model. We then develop such a MILP model and, in particular, introduce a new class of valid "band" inequalities, showing that they substantially strengthen the root bound. Building on this, we design a branch-and-check scheme: the master problem retains only the site variables together with one hypograph variable per cell and scenario, integer solutions are evaluated by an exact linear-time oracle, and the optimality cuts are problem-specific "upwind cuts". Computational results are reported on a ladder of square grids under a four-sector axial wind rose, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed improvements.

</details>


### [249] [Nonlinear Trajectory Optimization Models for Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles with Mobile Charging Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40201)
*Minsen Yuan,Amanuel Adane,James Humann,Yue Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种平滑非线性轨迹优化模型，用于移动充电站支持下的无人机任务调度与充电路径联合优化，通过带界近似误差的平滑化处理避免整数变量，从而大幅提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基础设施稀疏环境中，无人机需依托移动充电站实现持续自主作业。此时轨迹优化问题耦合了任务调度、何时何地充电以及充电可用性的地形可达性约束，现有方法难以高效求解。

Method: 构建平滑非线性轨迹优化模型：采用统一电池动力学模型支持非线性充电模式，并用析取约束刻画各模式的时间分配；进一步对析取约束做平滑近似，且近似误差有界，从而避免整数变量，可用平滑非线性优化算法高效求解。

Result: 在包含多空间分布任务、非线性恒流—恒压充电动力学及地形可达性约束的无人机任务上评估。与混合整数非线性规划相比，所提模型在提供高质量近似解的同时，将计算时间降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 平滑非线性建模结合有界误差近似可有效替代混合整数方法，在保证解质量的前提下显著提升移动充电支持下无人机轨迹优化的计算效率。

Abstract: Supporting Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) with mobile charging stations enables persistent UAV autonomy in infrastructure-sparse environments. In this setting, trajectory optimization for UAVs is challenging because it couples task scheduling with when and where to recharge, as well as terrain-access constraints on where charging is available. We propose a smooth nonlinear trajectory optimization model for UAV with mobile charging support. Compared with existing results, the proposed model allows nonlinear charging dynamics mode via a unified battery dynamics model with disjunctive constraints on the time allocated to each mode. Furthermore, it provides smooth approximations of the disjunctive constraints with bounded approximation errors. By avoiding integer variables, these approximations enable efficient solution using smooth nonlinear optimization algorithms. We evaluate the proposed model on UAV missions with multiple spatially distributed tasks, nonlinear constant-current--constant-voltage charging dynamics, and terrain-access constraints on mobile charging support. Compared with mixed-integer nonlinear programs, the proposed model provides high-quality approximate solutions while reducing the computation time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [250] [Open Capacity Pooling in Agentic Supply Chains: Coordination-Directed LLM Discovery and Distributed Re-optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40296)
*Yujia Xu,Walid Klibi,Benoit Montreuil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出"开放产能池化"问题，将外部产能搜索纳入供应链中断响应决策；用ADMM隐私协调、LLM索引检索与验证筛选完成配对，并给出最优性条件与实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链网络中断会耗尽自身产能，而外部闲置产能难以利用：现有模型为私有信息、供应商档案非结构化、报价在付出高昂接触成本前不可见。因此需要一种在不共享模型的前提下协调内部成员，并能高效发现、筛选和接入外部产能的机制。

Method: 将"开放产能池化"形式化为把网络成员资格作为中断响应决策的问题。用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）在不共享模型的情况下协调在位企业，由残余产能缺口指导搜索；用大语言模型（LLM）索引非结构化供应商档案，验证阶段揭示报价，采用约化成本筛选接纳报价，并以热启动ADMM重新优化。若所有合格报价以精确价格被揭示则该流程最优，否则为启发式。

Result: 在200个合成场景中，完全信息开放可挽回24.6%的产能稀缺成本。限定40次接触时，协调导向的需求选择加LLM排序可捕获该价值的32.0%，而无方向、无排序接触仅4.7%。LLM通读全部档案的表现与完整结构化登记册相差不到5个百分点，在10,000条记录中找出的兼容供应商数量是关键词检索的4倍。档案缺失的关键信息是每个供应商单一报价所覆盖的确切产能，请求其他产能则会失败；将报价登记入同一索引后，在无接触上限时将捕获价值从46.8%提升至98.6%，且在所测试的每个接触收费下净价值保持为正。

Conclusion: 外部产能利用的核心瓶颈在于报价信息的可见性与可匹配性，而非协调算法本身。ADMM私有协调加LLM检索能显著提升中断响应中的产能捕获效率，但档案对产能覆盖范围的不完整表述构成主要限制；将报价纳入统一索引可几乎消除该损失并维持正的净收益。

Abstract: Disruptions can exhaust a supply chain network's capacity, yet outside capacity is hard to use: incumbent models are private, provider profiles are unstructured, and offers stay hidden until costly engagement. We formulate open capacity pooling, making network membership a disruption-response decision. The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) coordinates incumbents without sharing models, and residual capacity gaps direct search over profiles indexed by a large language model (LLM). Verification reveals offers, reduced-cost screening admits them, and warm-started ADMM re-optimizes. The procedure is optimal if every eligible offer is revealed at exact prices, heuristic otherwise. Across 200 synthetic episodes, full-information opening recovers 24.6% of capacity-scarcity cost. With 40 contacts, coordination-directed need selection and LLM ranking capture 32.0% of this value, versus 4.7% for undirected, unranked contact. LLM reading of all profiles performs within five points of a complete structured registry and error-free extraction and finds four times as many compatible providers as keyword search among 10,000 records. What profiles omit is the exact capacity each provider's single offer covers, and requests for other capacity fail; registering offers in the same index raises captured value without a contact limit from 46.8% to 98.6%, keeping net value positive at every tested contact charge.

</details>


### [251] [ISS and integral ISS coincide for linear systems with bounded inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40348)
*Sahiba Arora,Philip Preußler,Felix Schwenninger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明线性系统中关于本质有界函数空间的ISS与积分ISS等价，并借此解决G. Weiss提出的一个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性系统中输入-状态稳定性（ISS）与积分输入-状态稳定性（integral ISS）在本质有界函数空间下是否等价，并回答G. Weiss关于L∞-容许控制算子下mild解连续性的公开问题。

Method: 结合近期关于Orlicz-容许性的结果与作者此前的对偶性结果进行证明。

Result: 证明了在线性系统中，关于本质有界函数空间的ISS与积分ISS是等价性质；并由此推出给定L∞-容许控制算子时线性系统的mild解是连续的。

Conclusion: 在线性系统框架下ISS与积分ISS对本质有界函数空间等价，且L∞-容许控制算子蕴含mild解连续性，从而解决了G. Weiss提出的问题。

Abstract: We show that input-to-state stability (ISS) and integral input-to-state stability (integral ISS) with respect to the space of essentially bounded functions are equivalent properties for linear systems. The proof combines recent results on Orlicz-admissibility and a duality result due to the authors. As a direct consequence, we show that, given an $L^\infty$-admissible control operator, the mild solutions of the linear system are continuous, resolving a question by G.\ Weiss.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Moment-based approximation of cell and face integrals in THINC/QQ reconstruction on unstructured grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38215)
*Young-Lin Yoo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出QMMS近似，用均值与方差闭合计算THINC/QQ中的界面常数与面平均，消除牛顿迭代和拓扑相关数值积分，重建阶段加速约1.85–2.12倍且结果基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THINC/QQ在非结构网格上需依赖拓扑的数值求积计算单元与面积分，并对界面常数进行牛顿迭代，实现复杂、运行开销大，亟需低成本的替代计算方式。

Method: 引入二次矩匹配Sigmoid（QMMS）近似，仅用二次场的均值与方差评估相关量，同时保留双曲正切重构剖面与BVD格式；采用斜率匹配高斯累积分布函数与矩匹配高斯模型，为界面常数和面平均提供闭式近似，从而移除牛顿迭代与拓扑相关的运行时求积。

Result: 单胞测试与九个二、三维基准算例表明，主要输运结构与激波结构与THINC/QQ高度一致，局部差异集中于间断、接触区与充分发展的剪切层附近；重建阶段计时显示THINC/QQ与QMMS的时间比在二维为2.12、三维为1.85。

Conclusion: QMMS在保持重构精度与主要流动特征的前提下降低了重建阶段成本，用通用的矩公式替代了拓扑相关的运行时积分评估。

Abstract: On unstructured grids, the tangent of hyperbola for interface capturing (THINC) method with quadratic surface representation and Gaussian quadrature (THINC/QQ) requires topology-dependent numerical quadrature for cell and face integrals and Newton iteration for the surface constant. This study introduces a quadratic moment-matched sigmoid (QMMS) approximation that evaluates these quantities from the mean and variance of the quadratic field while retaining the hyperbolic-tangent reconstruction profile and the boundary variation diminishing (BVD) formulation. A slope-matched Gaussian cumulative distribution function and a moment-matched Gaussian model provide closed form approximations for the surface constant and face averages, eliminating Newton iteration and topology-dependent runtime quadrature from these evaluations. Single-cell tests and nine two- and three-dimensional benchmarks assess reconstruction accuracy, flow-field differences, and computational cost. Across the tested cases, the principal transported and shock structures remain closely aligned with those obtained using THINC/QQ, with local differences concentrated mainly near discontinuities, contact regions, and developed shear layers. Reconstruction-stage profiling gives mean THINC/QQ-to-QMMS time ratios of 2.12 in two dimensions and 1.85 in three dimensions. QMMS therefore reduces reconstruction-stage cost while replacing topology-dependent runtime integral evaluation with a common moment-based formulation

</details>


### [253] [An Approximate Method for Modeling Plasmons on Graphene with Time- and Space-dependent Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38370)
*Kaleigh Rudge,Fadil Santosa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对随时间与空间变化的Drude权重下石墨烯表面等离激元传播，建立偏积分微分方程（PIDE）模型，发展正则摄动展开、基于Volterra积分方程与FFT的数值求解器，以及针对行波调制的解析变换解，并验证多种波动现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯的表面电导率（Drude权重）可被时间与空间调制，这为调控表面等离激元传播提供了新自由度；然而时空依赖的材料参数使传统方法难以高效、准确地建模其传播动力学，缺乏相应的解析与数值理论工具。

Method: 从Maxwell方程出发，将时空调制Drude权重下的电流密度演化写成偏积分微分方程（PIDE）。假设Drude权重为小振幅扰动，构造正则摄动展开，在适当Sobolev空间中证明逐阶积分方程的可解性与Fourier可逆性。提出结合Volterra积分方程表述与FFT的数值求解器，给出逐点误差估计与计算复杂度分析；针对行波Drude权重调制，利用围道积分与Laplace逆变换导出基于变换的解析解。

Result: 建立了逐阶积分方程的解存在性与可逆性理论结果，给出了数值方法的逐点误差估计与复杂度；数值模拟复现了行波、驻波以及指数增长的等离激元电流密度等多种波动现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为时空调制石墨烯上表面等离激元的传播提供了系统性的近似计算框架，兼具解析解与高效数值算法，并揭示调制可诱导丰富的等离激元动力学行为。

Abstract: This paper presents approximate computational methods for modeling surface plasmon propagation on a graphene sheet with time- and space-dependent material properties. Starting from Maxwell's equations, the evolution of current density under a spatially and temporally modulated Drude weight is formulated as a partial integro-differential equation (PIDE). Assuming small-amplitude perturbations of the Drude weight, a regular perturbation expansion is developed. The solvability and Fourier invertibility of the resulting order-by-order integral equations are established in appropriate Sobolev spaces. A numerical solver combining a Volterra integral equation formulation with the fast Fourier transform (FFT) is introduced, along with pointwise error estimates and computational complexity analysis. Additionally, an analytical transform-based solution using contour integration and Laplace inversion is derived for traveling-wave Drude weight modulations. Numerical simulations demonstrate diverse wave phenomena, including traveling, standing, and exponentially growing plasmonic current densities.

</details>


### [254] [An energy-based discontinuous Galerkin method for wave equations on complex geometric domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38378)
*Yangxin Fu,Yan Jiang,Siyang Wang,Lu Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出高阶切割能量间断伽辽金（CutEDG）方法，用于复杂域及静止材料界面上的二阶声波方程，通过幽灵罚稳定实现切割单元无关的稳定性与高阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂域和材料界面上求解声波方程时，传统贴体网格生成困难；采用非贴体笛卡尔网格会产生小切割单元，导致显式时间步长受限和刚度矩阵条件数恶化，亟需在保持原有数值通量结构的前提下解决该问题。

Method: 基于非贴体笛卡尔网格构造高阶CutEDG格式；将幽灵罚稳定项直接引入定义EDG能量的局部双线性形式中，保持原有空间耦合与数值通量结构不变；建立半离散能量稳定性与先验误差估计，并推广至含波速间断的界面问题。

Result: 理论分析证明了半离散能量稳定性，并给出保持高阶精度的先验误差估计；数值实验验证了预测的收敛阶，且对任意小切割单元具有鲁棒性；显式时间步长限制由背景网格尺寸决定而非最小物理切割单元，稳定后的能量Gram矩阵条件数与切割无关。

Conclusion: CutEDG方法在非贴体网格上实现了二阶声波方程的高阶、稳定、鲁棒求解，适用于复杂域与静止材料界面问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a high-order cut energy-based discontinuous Galerkin (CutEDG) method for second-order acoustic wave equations on complex domains and across stationary material interfaces. The method employs unfitted Carte- sian meshes and addresses the small-cut-cell problem by introducing ghost-penalty stabilization directly into the local bilinear forms defining the EDG energy, while leaving the original spatial coupling and numerical-flux structure unchanged. We establish semidiscrete energy stability and a priori error estimates showing that high-order accuracy is retained. The formulation is further extended to interface problems with discontinuous wave speeds. Numerical experiments confirm the pre- dicted convergence rates and demonstrate robustness with respect to arbitrarily small cut cells. In particular, the explicit time-step restriction is governed by the background mesh size rather than the smallest physical cut cell, and the stabilized energy Gram matrices exhibit cut-independent conditioning.

</details>


### [255] [The Mode of Null-A: Compositional Computation of a Generalized Inverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38497)
*Barak A. Pearlmutter,Jeffrey Mark Siskind*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出 Null-A 前像自动微分（AD）算法，能高效计算仿射空间经矩阵乘积的仿射前像，支持非方阵 Jacobian 及计算过程中活动变量数变化的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆 AD 通常只能求解 $J\acute{\mathbf{x}}^*=\acute{\mathbf{y}}^*$ 或 $J^T\grave{\mathbf{y}}^*=\grave{\mathbf{x}}^*$ 这样的单点逆问题，且要求矩阵为方阵、计算中活动变量数固定。实际 AD 中 Jacobian 是线性化基本数值运算矩阵的乘积，且非方阵、变量数会增减，因此需要一个更一般的框架来计算整个仿射前像空间。

Method: 将矩阵乘积 $J=J_{T-1}\cdots J_0$ 的前像计算分解为按序引入各 $J_t$ 的组合式、准局部计算；用一种适合高效前像计算的仿射空间表示，逐次通过各 $J_t$ 传播，从而得到最大的输入仿射空间 $\mathbf{X}$ 满足 $J\mathbf{x}\in\mathbf{Y}$。允许 $J_t$ 为非方阵，对应程序活动变量数在计算中膨胀与收缩。

Result: 当 $J$ 为方阵且初始仿射空间为单点时，方法退化为传统逆；更一般情形下整个仿射空间提供了可依问题定制的自由度。在 CPU 上验证了以线性化标量一元/二元数值函数为 $J_t$ 的小规模问题，在 GPU 上验证了以卷积、注意力等线性化聚合数组运算为 $J_t$ 的较大规模问题。

Conclusion: Null-A 前像模式 AD 推广了逆 AD：它不要求 $J_t$ 为方阵，能处理活动变量数动态变化，并返回完整仿射前像空间而非单点解，为利用问题特定自由度提供了通用且高效的机制。

Abstract: We present a novel algorithm for calculating the preimage of an affine space through a product ${J}={J}_{T-1}\cdots{J}_0$ of matrices ${J}_t$ of special form: finding the largest input space $\mathbf{X}$ such that $\mathbf{x}\in\mathbf{X}$ implies ${J}\mathbf{x}\in \mathbf{Y}$, where $\mathbf{Y}$ is a given output affine space. These special matrices arise in AD, where the Jacobians $J$ describing the linearized computation have precisely this structure: the product of a series of linearized primitive numeric operations. This allows us to use the new algorithm to formulate Null-A mode preimage AD, which finds the affine preimage through the Jacobian or Jacobian transpose of a numeric computation. This is a generalization of the inverse AD problem of solving ${J}\acute{\mathbf{x}}^{\ast}=\acute{\mathbf{y}}^{\ast}$ or ${J}^{T}\grave{\mathbf{y}}^{\ast}=\grave{\mathbf{x}}^{\ast}$. The key is to represent affine spaces in a fashion which lends itself to efficient preimage calculation, in a compositional and quasi-local fashion, through a succession of matrices ${J}_t$.
  Unlike previous methods, Null-A preimage mode AD allows the ${J}_t$ matrices to be non-square, corresponding to a computer program whose number of active variables swells and shrinks during the computation. When ${J}$ is square and the initial affine space is a single point, this finds the conventional inverse. But in the more general case, having the entire affine space provides freedom which can be leveraged in a problem-specific manner. We apply the method to small problems on-CPU where the $J_t$ are linearized scalar unary or binary numeric functions; and to larger problems on-GPU where the $J_t$ are linearized aggregate array operations like convolution and attention.

</details>


### [256] [Worst-Case Completion of Tensors with Approximately Few ANOVA Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38749)
*Simon Foucart,Jingchun Shao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从最坏情况视角研究从不完整元素信息中补全张量的问题，假设张量的低阶ANOVA项占主导，借助最优恢复理论给出理论解，并提出基于FFT的存储友好方法实现低阶ANOVA投影及高效计算正则项与极值特征值，在合成张量和真实数据集上验证了精度与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中张量的低阶ANOVA项通常占主导，因此需要在该假设下从部分已知元素补全张量；已有最优恢复理论虽能提供理论解，但其涉及半定规划的构造因张量维度巨大而无法直接应用，亟需可扩展的计算方案。

Method: 采用最坏情况视角与最优恢复（Optimal Recovery）理论框架给出补全问题的理论解；针对高维导致半定规划不可行的问题，提出基于快速傅里叶变换（FFT）的存储友好方法生成低阶ANOVA投影，并利用补全问题的特殊性高效计算正则化项与极值特征值。

Result: 在合成张量与真实世界数据集上的数值实验表明，所提出的FFT方法具有较高的补全精度和良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于FFT的补全方法能够克服传统半定规划构造在高维张量中的维度瓶颈，在保证精度的同时实现可扩展的张量补全。

Abstract: In this article, the problem of completing a tensor from some incomplete knowledge of its entries is treated by adopting a worst-case perspective, given the realistic assumption that the tensor's low-order ANOVA terms are dominant. We survey and leverage some recent all-purpose results from the field of Optimal Recovery to provide solutions on a theoretical level. But the accompanying constructions of optimal completion procedures, which often feature semidefinite programs, are not directly applicable in the tensor case due to the huge dimensions involved. To resolve the issue, we put forward a storage-friendly way to produce low-order ANOVA projections based on the fast Fourier transform (FFT), while exploiting the specificities of the completion problem to efficiently compute regularizers and extremal eigenvalues. Numerical experiments on synthetic tensors and real-world datasets demonstrate the accuracy and scalability of our FFT-based method.

</details>


### [257] [A fully convergent fixed-point fast sweeping method with the WENO-JS local solver for steady state of hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38841)
*Liang Li,Jun Zhu,Yong-Tao Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文回到经典WENO-JS局部求解器框架，提出一种新的五阶全收敛不动点快速扫描方法：通过"冻结权重"技术，在迭代残差稳定后冻结非线性权重，并设计了简单稳健的残差稳定判据与冻结时机判定方法，从而将双曲守恒律稳态问题的迭代残差驱动至舍入误差水平，实现完全收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶不动点快速扫描方法结合经典WENO-JS局部求解器时，迭代残差往往难以降至舍入误差，无法实现完全收敛。已有的解决方案是采用基于不等尺寸子模板的非传统WENO局部求解器，但这类格式更复杂、计算成本更高。因此需要一种既保持经典WENO-JS简单高效、又能实现完全收敛的新方法。

Method: 提出新的五阶全收敛不动点快速扫描方法，仍采用经典WENO-JS局部求解器。基于近期关于解间断附近非线性加权过程的研究，引入冻结权重技术：当快速扫描迭代中的残差序列稳定后即冻结非线性权重，以避免非线性权重的非必要调整。与已有冻结权重工作不同，本文设计了简单而稳健的方法来判定迭代残差的稳定性，并确定快速扫描中冻结非线性权重的迭代步。

Result: 在广泛的二维稳态挑战性问题上的大量数值实验表明，与以往采用五阶WENO-JS局部求解器的快速扫描方法不同，新格式能够持续地将迭代残差驱动至舍入误差水平，实现完全收敛。

Conclusion: 通过冻结权重技术，可在保持经典WENO-JS局部求解器简洁性与计算效率的同时，解决高阶不动点快速扫描方法残差难以收敛至舍入误差的问题，为双曲守恒律稳态问题提供了一种全收敛的高阶快速扫描格式。

Abstract: The fixed-point fast sweeping methods with weighted essentially non-oscillatory (WENO) local solvers are a class of efficient and high-order accuracy numerical methods for solving steady-state solutions of hyperbolic conservation laws. However, with the classical WENO-JS local solver, the iteration residue of high-order fixed-point fast sweeping scheme often has difficulty to settle down to round-off errors. To achieve the full convergence in a fast sweeping method, the fixed-point fast sweeping methods with non-traditional WENO local solvers based on unequal-sized substencils were designed. However, the WENO schemes based on unequal-sized stencils are more complex and in general more expensive in computational costs than the classical WENO-JS schemes. In this paper, we go back to the classical WENO-JS local solver and develop a new fully convergent fifth-order fixed-point fast sweeping method for solving steady-state problems of hyperbolic conservation laws. Based on recent studies on the nonlinear weighting process around discontinuities of solution, we apply the technique of frozen weights for avoiding unnecessary adjustment of nonlinear weights in the WENO-JS local solver, which freezes the nonlinear weights once the residual sequence in the fast sweeping iterations has stabilized. Different from the existing work on frozen weights, we design a simple and robust approach to judge stabilization of iteration residues and determine the iteration step when the nonlinear weights are frozen in the fast sweeping method. Extensive numerical experiments on a wide range of challenging two-dimensional steady-state problems demonstrate that, unlike the previous fast sweeping method with the fifth-order WENO-JS local solver, the proposed new scheme consistently drives the iteration residues to round-off errors and achieves the full convergence.

</details>


### [258] [A Kernel-Aware Two-Grid Preconditioner for Cell-Centered Nearly Incompressible Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38871)
*Shubin Fu,Xiang Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对弱对称多点应力离散化中因Lamé比增大而难以预条件的位移Schur补系统，提出一种双网格预条件器：结合常规位移粗子空间与位于细网格体积核内的离散旋度子空间，并配以对称顶点块光滑，得到与Lamé比无关的条件数界，2D/3D实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几乎不可压缩弹性问题常用混合方法离散以避免锁死，但所得鞍点系统求解代价高。虽可通过在顶点交互区域独立消去应力与旋转未知量，得到仅含单元中心位移的对称正定系统，规模虽减小，但其Schur补含参数依赖，随Lamé比增大而难以预条件；且标准单元中心插值不必保持体积核，在近不可压缩时引入能量放大。

Method: 采用弱对称多点应力离散化，在顶点交互区域独立消去应力和旋转未知量，得到仅含单元中心位移的对称正定系统。分析其能量结构，证明其由一致强制的剪切部分与占优的半正定体积部分组成。基于该结构设计双网格预条件器：融合两个互补粗子空间——常规位移子空间与恰好位于细网格体积核中的离散旋度子空间；并用对称顶点块光滑构成纯位移求解器。

Result: 能量分裂与核相容分解对每个固定网格对给出与Lamé比无关的条件数界。二维与三维实验证实了局部消去离散化的逼近性质，并表明核修正可防止单独位移粗子空间的性能退化，且在材料系数不连续时同样有效。

Conclusion: 该双网格预条件器通过引入与体积核相容的离散旋度粗子空间，有效克服了近不可压缩下位移Schur补的预条件困难，实现Lamé比无关的条件数界，对连续及不连续材料系数均适用。

Abstract: Nearly incompressible elasticity is often discretized by mixed methods to avoid locking, but the resulting saddle-point systems can be expensive to solve. We consider a weakly symmetric multipoint stress discretization whose stress and rotation unknowns can be eliminated independently over vertex interaction regions, leaving a symmetric positive definite system for cell-centered displacement only. Although smaller, the reduced system contains a parameter-dependent Schur complement that becomes difficult to precondition as the Lamé ratio increases. We show that its energy consists of a uniformly coercive shear part and a dominant semidefinite volumetric part. Standard cell-centered interpolation need not preserve the volumetric kernel and can introduce energy amplified near incompressibility. Motivated by this structure, we develop a two-grid preconditioner combining two complementary coarse subspaces: a conventional displacement subspace and a discrete-curl subspace lying exactly in the fine-grid volumetric kernel. Symmetric vertex-patch smoothing completes the displacement-only solver. The energy splitting and kernel-compatible decompositions yield a condition-number bound independent of the Lamé ratio for each fixed grid pair. Two- and three-dimensional experiments confirm the approximation properties of the locally eliminated discretization and show that the kernel correction prevents the deterioration of the displacement coarse subspace alone, also for discontinuous material coefficients.

</details>


### [259] [A positive-conservative Fourier optimization method for ground states of Bose--Einstein condensates with higher-order interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38965)
*Bo Lin,Xinran Ruan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出正保守傅里叶优化(PCFO)方法，求解含高阶相互作用的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体基态，通过凸正则化、傅里叶伪谱离散与两阶段优化实现高精度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态问题存在凸密度表述，但零密度附近的奇异行为导致高精度计算困难。

Method: 引入密度能量的凸正则化，证明其在计算域上当正则化参数趋于零时的Γ-收敛；采用具有质量守恒与节点正性的傅里叶伪谱法离散正则化问题；对约束优化问题先用加速一阶方法进行主能量下降，再用牛顿精化降低最优性残差。

Result: 数值实验显示正则化问题具谱型收敛；量化了正则化参数对精度与空间分辨率的影响；验证了两阶段优化方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PCFO方法能有效处理零密度奇异性，实现含高阶相互作用BEC基态的高精度计算。

Abstract: We develop a positive-conservative Fourier optimization (PCFO) method for computing ground states of Bose--Einstein condensates with higher-order interactions. The ground-state problem admits a convex density formulation, but the singular behavior near zero density poses difficulties for high-accuracy computation. We introduce convex regularizations of the density energy and establish their $Γ$-convergence on the computational domain as the regularization parameters vanish. The regularized problem is discretized by a Fourier pseudospectral method with mass conservation and nodal positivity. For the resulting constrained optimization problem, we use an accelerated first-order method for the main energy reduction, followed by a Newton refinement to reduce the remaining optimality residual. Numerical experiments show spectral-type convergence for regularized problems, quantify the effects of the regularization parameters on accuracy and spatial resolution, and demonstrate the effectiveness of the two-stage optimization method.

</details>


### [260] [Structure-preserving Fourier Neural Operators for Long-time Cahn-Hilliard Dynamics under Coarse Temporal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39053)
*Yingli Li,Y. C. Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Cahn-Hilliard方程长时预测中误差累积问题，提出两阶段保结构Fourier神经算子学习框架：Stage I的bcFNO通过幅度惩罚稳定长时预测，Stage II的spFNO加入结构函数正则化以校正晚期粗化统计，实现长期动力学的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard动力学（尤其晚期粗化阶段）的长时精确预测需要跨越大规模物理时间的仿真，计算成本高昂。现有机器学习代理模型虽在单步预测区间内准确，但反复迭代应用时小误差会累积，导致长时预测出现大数值误差。亟需一种能抑制误差累积、保持物理结构的算子学习方法。

Method: 识别Cahn-Hilliard粗化动力学的结构函数，提出两阶段保结构FNO框架。Stage I：界约束符合的FNO（bcFNO），采用依赖幅度的软惩罚项，抑制大幅值偏离。Stage II：在保留界约束目标的基础上，引入结构函数正则化，得到保结构FNO（spFNO），用于校正晚期粗化统计量。

Result: 在不同空间分辨率和参考求解器时间步上的数值实验表明：bcFNO可在不改变FNO低在线成本的前提下，通过抑制大幅值偏移稳定长时预测；结构函数项持续减小归一化结构函数的偏差，并进一步校正相关晚期粗化量，包括L^3(t)增长趋势和标度坍缩。

Conclusion: 将物理原理分阶段嵌入算子学习的实现方式，可推广至其他具有统计标度特征的多尺度系统（如泛函化Cahn-Hilliard方程和湍流），前提是状态约束与统计可观测量需适配相应的控制动力学。

Abstract: Accurate long-time prediction of Cahn-Hilliard dynamics, particularly in the late-stage coarsening regime, is computationally demanding because it requires simulations over extended physical time. Various machine learning approaches have been developed to speed up the simulations through efficient surrogate evaluations. While the learned map can be accurate over a single learned interval, small prediction error will accumulate when the map is repeatedly applied in a long-time prediction, leading to large numerical errors. In this work, we identify the structure function for the coarsening dynamics of the Cahn-Hilliard equation and propose a two-stage structure-preserving Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learning framework. Stage~I introduces a bound-conforming FNO (bcFNO) with a soft, magnitude-dependent amplitude penalty. Stage~II retains the bound-conforming objective and supplements it with structure-function regularization, yielding a structure-preserving FNO (spFNO) that corrects late-stage coarsening statistics. Numerical experiments on the various spatial resolutions and reference-solver time steps suggest that the bcFNO stabilizes the long-time prediction by suppressing large amplitude excursions, without changing the low online cost of the FNO. Furthermore, the structure-function term consistently reduces discrepancies in the normalized structure function and provides further correction in associated late-stage coarsening quantities, including the $L^3(t)$ growth trend and the scaling collapse. These results indicate that our multi-stage implementation of physical principles in operator learning could be applied to other multiscale systems with statistical scaling, such as the functionalized Cahn--Hilliard equations and turbulence, provided that the state constraints and statistical observables are adapted to the governing dynamics.

</details>


### [261] [Quantum Frozen--Oseen homotopy analysis method with LCHS for solving nonlinear partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39170)
*Jinhao Yuan,Leyu Chen,Tiegang Liu,Kun Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出FOQHAM框架，用冻结Fréchet导数与同伦层级线性化非线性PDE，并通过LCHS实现量子线性演化传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性PDE是计算流体力学的核心，但细网格上求解非线性输运计算代价高；量子线性演化算法虽可扩展，却无法直接传播非线性耦合。

Method: 在指定流场剖面上冻结完整Fréchet导数（保留输运与剖面梯度耦合），通过乘积提升将各指定有限阶同伦层级闭合为固定仿射线性系统，并用Hamiltonian模拟的线性组合（LCHS）实现传播，无需外层同伦迭代或剖面更新；对空间半离散方程给出局部逼近界与有限阶层级的精确闭合性。

Result: 在Burgers、KdV、Zakharov–Kuznetsov以及一、二维等温可压缩Navier–Stokes方程上的经典测试表明，在测试区间内可实现精确的非迭代逼近。

Conclusion: 该框架将非线性PDE逼近与量子线性演化相衔接，提供了通向量子加速的条件性路径。

Abstract: Nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) underpin computational fluid dynamics, yet resolving nonlinear transport on fine grids remains computationally demanding. Quantum linear-evolution algorithms offer a possible route to large-scale simulation but cannot directly propagate nonlinear coupling. Here we develop a Frozen--Oseen quantum homotopy method (FOQHAM) framework that links transport-aware auxiliary-operator selection to the size of the resulting linear representation. We freeze the full Fréchet derivative at a prescribed flow profile, retaining transport and profile-gradient coupling and close each prescribed finite-order homotopy hierarchy as a fixed affine linear system by product lifting. We formulate its propagation through Linear Combinations of Hamiltonian Simulations (LCHS), without outer homotopy iterations or profile updates. For spatially semidiscrete equations, we establish local approximation bounds and exact closure of the finite-order hierarchy. Classical tests on Burgers, Korteweg--de Vries, Zakharov--Kuznetsov, and one- and two-dimensional isothermal compressible Navier--Stokes equations provide numerical evidence of accurate non-iterative approximations in the tested regimes. The framework thus connects nonlinear PDE approximation to quantum linear evolution and offers a conditional path toward quantum acceleration.

</details>


### [262] [Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund inequalities for hemispherical $t$-designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39171)
*Chao Li,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在半球上引入新的 $\mathbb{L}_2$-正交多项式，建立偶/奇球面多项式的 $\mathbb{L}_1$ Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund 不等式，给出半球 $t$-design 的变分刻画，并证明当 $n\geq C_d t^d$ 时存在含 $n$ 个点的半球 $t$-design。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半球 $t$-design 可作为半球上等权重的求积公式，对次数不超过 $t$ 的多项式精确。已有研究多集中于整球面，半球上缺乏合适的正交多项式工具与相应的不等式，因而无法给出半球 $t$-design 的存在性与规模估计。

Method: 构造半球 $\mathbb{S}^d_+$ 上一组新的 $\mathbb{L}_2$-正交多项式；对偶、奇球面多项式建立 $\mathbb{L}_1$ Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund 不等式；利用这些正交多项式给出半球 $t$-design 的变分刻画；结合不等式与正交多项式进行存在性构造与计数论证。

Result: 得到了半球上新的 $\mathbb{L}_2$-正交多项式及 $\mathbb{L}_1$ Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund 不等式；给出了半球 $t$-design 的变分刻画；证明对每个 $n\geq C_d t^d$ 均存在含 $n$ 个点的半球 $t$-design，其中 $C_d$ 仅依赖于 $d$。

Conclusion: 该工作为半球上的等权重求积规则提供了理论工具，确立了半球 $t$-design 的存在性及其点数下界与 $t^d$ 同阶的结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a new set of $\mathbb{L}_2$-orthogonal polynomials on the hemisphere $\mathbb{S}^d_+:=\{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d+1}:\|\mathbf{x}\|=1, \mathbf{x}\cdot\mathbf{e}_{d+1}\geq0\}$ and establish $\mathbb{L}_1$ Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund inequalities on the hemisphere for even and odd spherical polynomials. Hemispherical $t$-designs provide equal weight cubature rules on the hemisphere which are exact for polynomials up to degree $t$. We propose variational characterizations of hemispherical $t$-designs by the $\mathbb{L}_2$-orthogonal polynomials. Moreover, based on the Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund inequalities and the $\mathbb{L}_2$-orthogonal polynomials, we prove that for each $n\geq C_d t^d$, there exists a hemispherical $t$-design on the hemisphere with $n$ points, where $C_d$ is a constant depending only on $d$.

</details>


### [263] [Fast and Stable Harmonic Approximation from Ill-distributed Data using Moving Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39200)
*Ralf Hielscher,Tim Pöschl,Erik Wünsche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出 HAMLS 方法，通过移动最小二乘将散乱数据过渡到求积网格，再用 FFT 完成全局调和逼近，兼顾稳定性与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局调和展开方法依赖节点几何，需要足够小的填充距离；单个大空洞会导致病态与运行时间过长，亟需一种对节点分布不敏感、稳定且快速的全局逼近方案。

Method: HAMLS 分两阶段：先用移动最小二乘（MLS）将散乱数据映射到求积网格，把一个大全局问题分解为多个可单独正则化的小局部问题；再在网格上通过快速傅里叶变换求积，得到全局调和逼近，全程非迭代。

Result: 对在分布良好、填充距离为 h 的节点上采样的足够光滑函数，L² 误差被界定为求积网格上精确值所得调和逼近的误差加上 O(h^{K+1}) 项，K 为 MLS 所用多项式次数；在 S² 上对病态分布节点的实验中，HAMLS 误差与全局最小二乘相当，但速度显著更快，带宽越大优势越明显。

Conclusion: HAMLS 通过局部 MLS 加 FFT 求积的两阶段设计，避免了病态全局求解，在超定与欠定情形下均表现良好，为大带宽、非均匀采样下的调和逼近提供了稳定高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We consider harmonic approximation on the torus $\mathbb{T}^d$, the sphere $\mathcal{S}^d$, and the rotation group $\mathrm{SO}(3)$. While several global approaches are available for computing a harmonic expansion from scattered data, their stability and, especially, their runtime depend strongly on the geometry of the nodes, in particular on a sufficiently small fill distance. Even a single large hole in the data may cause instability and long runtimes.
  We propose harmonic approximation via moving least squares (HAMLS), a global harmonic approximation scheme that avoids the ill-conditioned global solve and works well in both the overdetermined and the underdetermined settings. The main idea is an intermediate transition from the scattered data to a quadrature grid, which is realized via moving least squares (MLS). This replaces one large global problem by many small local ones that can be regularized individually. The global harmonic approximation is then obtained via quadrature using a fast Fourier transform. This makes the global stage fast, stable and non-iterative. For sufficiently smooth functions sampled at well-distributed nodes with small fill distance $h$, we bound the resulting $\mathrm{L}^2$-error by the error of the harmonic approximation obtained from exact values on the quadrature grid, plus a term of order $h^{K+1}$, where $K$ is the polynomial degree employed in the MLS step. Numerical experiments on $\mathcal{S}^2$ with ill-distributed nodes show that HAMLS achieves errors comparable to those of global least-squares approximation, while being significantly faster, especially for larger bandwidths.

</details>


### [264] [On bounds for rank growth of iterands in conjugate gradients for Lyapunov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39308)
*Jana Lungová,Martin Plešinger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Lyapunov方程AX+XA^T=F的求解，其中A为对称正定稀疏矩阵、F为对称低秩矩阵，解X对称但通常稠密，可用低秩矩阵近似。采用共轭梯度法的矩阵重构（MCG）配合低秩算术（LRA）进行迭代求解，重点关注迭代过程中解近似X_ℓ、残差R_ℓ和方向向量P_ℓ的秩行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当n很大时，稠密解X无法直接计算，但可通过低秩算术（LRA）访问。因此需要研究利用共轭梯度法的矩阵重构（MCG）求解该Lyapunov方程时，低秩近似的数值行为与可行性。

Method: 将共轭梯度法进行矩阵重构（MCG），结合低秩算术（LRA）迭代求解Lyapunov方程AX+XA^T=F，并分析迭代过程中解近似X_ℓ、残差R_ℓ及方向向量P_ℓ的秩变化。

Result: Abstract未给出具体数值结果，主要关注并考察迭代过程中X_ℓ、R_ℓ、P_ℓ的秩行为。

Conclusion: 通过MCG与LRA可对大规模Lyapunov方程进行低秩近似求解，其解近似、残差与方向向量的秩在迭代过程中的表现是研究的核心关注点。

Abstract: We focus on solving the Lyapunov equation $AX + XA^T = F$, where $A$, $X$ and $F$ are square matrices, $A$ is symmetric positive definite (SPD) and sparse, and $F$ is symmetric and of a low-rank. The solution $X$ is then also symmetric and in general dense, but it can be approximated by a low-rank matrix. If $n$ is large, $X$ cannot be computed directly, but it is accessible by using the so-called low-rank arithmetics (LRA). The equation is solved by matrix reformulation of the method of conjugate gradients (MCG). We are interested in the behavior of ranks of the solution approximations $X_\ell$, residuals $R_\ell$, and direction vectors $P_\ell$ during iterations.

</details>


### [265] [Well-Conditioned Birkhoff-Collocation Methods for Elliptic-type Problems in Multiple Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39353)
*Shunchang Li,Zixuan Gao,Yujian Jiao,Li-Lian Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 基于Legendre-Gauss-Lobatto点的Birkhoff插值，构造多维谱配置法的稳定对角化与预条件子，解决二阶椭圆型问题的病态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯型点Birkhoff插值的配置法在一维初边值问题中条件良好，但其构造无法直接推广到多维。多维配置法用于二阶椭圆型问题时长期存在严重病态（稠密、非对称、条件数巨大），限制了谱配置法在实际多维问题中的应用。

Method: 关键观察：由Legendre-Gauss-Lobatto点Birkhoff插值构造的二阶微分矩阵及其逆（伪谱积分矩阵PSIM）均相似于对称负定矩阵。据此可对稠密、非对称、病态的微分与积分矩阵实现稳定对角化，即使配置点数达数千；并由此构造高效的多维Birkhoff预条件子。对变系数问题，系数被直接纳入对角化与预条件子构造，以适应强各向异性、高对比度、振荡及退化椭圆算子。

Result: 给出了谱分析，并进行了大量二维与三维数值实验：条件数显著降低，GMRES收敛几乎与多项式次数无关，同时保持高阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的Birkhoff配置格式使多维谱配置法对具有挑战性的椭圆问题变得实用。

Abstract: Collocation methods based on Birkhoff interpolation at Gaussian-type points are well-conditioned for one-dimensional initial and boundary value problems [Wang et al., {\em SIAM J. Sci. Comput.} 36 (2014)], but the underlying construction does not extend directly to multiple dimensions. We address the long-standing ill-conditioning of multidimensional collocation methods for second-order elliptic-type problems. The key observation is that the second-order differentiation matrix and its inverse, the pseudospectral integration matrix (PSIM) constructed from Birkhoff interpolation at Legendre-Gauss-Lobatto points, are both similar to symmetric negative definite matrices. This enables stable diagonalisation of the dense, non-symmetric and ill-conditioned differentiation and integration matrices, even for thousands of collocation points, and leads to efficient multidimensional Birkhoff preconditioners. For variable-coefficient problems, the coefficients are incorporated directly into the diagonalisation and preconditioner construction, which is essential for highly anisotropic, high-contrast, oscillatory and degenerate elliptic operators. We provide spectral analysis and extensive two- and three-dimensional numerical experiments, demonstrating substantial reductions in condition numbers and nearly polynomial-degree-independent GMRES convergence while retaining high-order accuracy. The resulting Birkhoff-collocation schemes make multidimensional spectral collocation methods practical for challenging elliptic problems.

</details>


### [266] [Mesh-Free Numerical Approximation of the Biharmonic Equation via Optimized Kolmogorov--Arnold Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39469)
*B. Veena S. N. Rao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于KAN-PINN的无网格框架求解四阶椭圆问题（双调和方程），在单位方形光滑基准上相对L2误差达1.593e-5，训练损失3.065e-6，仅用7,801个可训练参数。
